\bm{x}_{l_{1}},\bm{y}^{\prime}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
, we have
\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{y}^{\prime}\|\leq 4\zeta_{1}
. Since (
3.1
) and
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{1}}{10}\gg 1
,

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{x}\|+1)+\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{y}^{\prime}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{x}\|+\|\bm{y}-\bm{y}^{\prime}\|)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{y}^{\prime}\|)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-4\zeta_{1})-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2.

(4.78)

From (
4.71
),
\tilde{\zeta}_{1}\gg\zeta_{1}
,
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho<\rho^{\prime}+1
and
N_{1}\gg 1
, we also get

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-4\zeta_{1})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{80\rho\zeta_{1}}{\tilde{\zeta}_{1}\log N_{1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1).

(4.79)

Combining (
4.69
), (
Case 2-3:
)–(
4.79
),
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho<\rho^{\prime}+1
and
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{1}}{10}\gg 1
gives

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha\left(1-\frac{20\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)}.

This finishes the proof.
∎

Step 3

:
Estimates of general
1
-good
\Lambda
.

In this final step, we complete the verification of property
\mathscr{P}_{1}
. Recall that a finite set
\Lambda\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}
is called 1-
{\rm good}
if it satisfies the following conditions:

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\Lambda\cap Q_{0}\cap\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}\neq\emptyset\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda,\\
\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda\}\cap Q_{1}=\emptyset.\end{array}\right.

To establish
\mathscr{P}_{1}
, we will synthesize three key components: (a) The norm estimates of
\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}}^{-1}
obtained previously; (b) Schur’s test for operator bounds; (c) The resolvent identity technique.

Theorem 4.6
.

If
\Lambda
is
1
-
{\rm good}
, then

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta_{0}^{-3}\sup_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle<\delta_{1}^{-3},

(4.82)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle<e^{-\alpha_{1}\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|}\ \text{for $\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq 10\tilde{\zeta}_{1}$,}

(4.83)

where
\alpha_{1}=\frac{3}{4}\alpha\left(1-\frac{50\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}\right)
is defined in (
4.19
).

Proof.

First, we prove (
4.82
) by Schur’s test. Define

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{1}=\left\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \Lambda\cap\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}\cap Q_{0}\neq\emptyset\right\}\subset P_{1}.

From the definition of
1
-good set (cf. (
4.2
)), we have
\widetilde{P}_{1}\cap Q_{1}=\emptyset
. For every
\bm{w}\in\Lambda
, define its block in
\Lambda
:

\displaystyle O(\bm{w})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{w})\cap\Lambda,&\text{if $\bm{w}\notin\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}$,}\\
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1},&\text{if $\bm{w}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}$ for some $\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}\setminus Q_{1}$.}\end{array}\right.

Since
\Lambda
is
1
-good,
\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}
exists for each
\bm{x}\in\Lambda
. Hence we can define
\mathcal{L}
and
\mathcal{K}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y}),&\text{for $\bm{x}\in\Lambda$ and $\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})$,}\\
0,&\text{else,}\end{array}\right.

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\sum\limits_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})}\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{z})\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y}),&\text{for $\bm{x}\in\Lambda$ and $\bm{y}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{x})$,}\\
0,&\text{else}.\end{array}\right.

Direct computations shows

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}=\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\varepsilon\mathcal{K}.

Next, we will prove the invertibility of
\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\varepsilon\mathcal{K}
through the following steps: (a) Schur’s test yields
\|\mathcal{K}\|\leq D\left(\frac{5}{16}\alpha\right)<+\infty
, where
D(\eta)
is defined in (
2.1
); (b) Neumann series expansion is valid for
\|\varepsilon\mathcal{K}\|<1
. This ensures the existence of
\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}=(\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\varepsilon\mathcal{K})^{-1}\mathcal{L}
.

Now, we need to estimate
\sup_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|
and
\sup_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|
respectively for controlling
\|\mathcal{K}\|
. We estimate
|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|
first. We divide the discussion into three cases:

Case 1
:

\bm{x}\notin\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
and
\bm{y}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{x})
. In this case, we have
O(\bm{x})
is
0
-good. From (
1.2
), (
4.26
) and (
4.27
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq({\rm I})+({\rm II}),

(4.90)

where

\displaystyle({\rm I})=\sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus\{\bm{x}\}}e^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{z}\|)}\cdot e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{y}\|)}

and

\displaystyle({\rm II})=(2\kappa_{1}^{-1}\delta_{0}^{-2})\cdot e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}.

For
({\rm I})
, by (
3.1
) and
\bm{y}\notin O(\bm{x})
(
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{1}{2}N_{1}\gg 1
), we get

\displaystyle({\rm I})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus\{\bm{x}\}}e^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{z}\|)}\cdot e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{y}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)+\frac{3}{4}\alpha\cdot C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2}\sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus\{\bm{x}\}}e^{-\frac{\alpha}{10}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{y}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)\cdot e^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)+\frac{3}{4}\alpha\cdot C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)},

(4.91)

where
D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)
is defined in (
2.1
). For
({\rm II})
, since
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{1}{2}N_{1}\gg 1
, (
4.3
) and
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho
, we obtain

\displaystyle({\rm II})

\displaystyle=2\kappa_{1}^{-1}\delta_{0}^{-2}e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\right)}\leq\frac{1}{2}e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\right)}.

(4.92)

Therefore, from (
4.90
), (
4.91
) and (
4.92
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\right)}.

(4.93)

Case 2
:

\bm{x}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
for some
\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}\setminus Q_{1}
and
\bm{y}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{x})
. In this case, we define
X:=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{1}}{9}}(\bm{k})\subset O(\bm{x})
. Hence

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq({\rm III})+({\rm IV}),

(4.94)

where

\displaystyle({\rm III})=\sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus X}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|

and

\displaystyle({\rm IV})=\sum_{\bm{z}\in X}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|.

For
({\rm III})
, since (
1.2
), (
3.1
),
\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus X
(
\|\bm{z}-\bm{x}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{1}}{10}
),
\bm{y}\notin O(\bm{x})
(
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{1}}{3}
) and (
4.68
), we have

\displaystyle({\rm III})=

\displaystyle\ \ \sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus X}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus X}e^{-\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{z}\|)}\cdot e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{y}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ e^{-\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)+\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\cdot C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2}\sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus X}e^{-\frac{1}{10}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{y}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)\cdot e^{-\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)+\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\cdot C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \frac{1}{2}e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)},

(4.95)

where
D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)
is defined in (
2.1
). For
({\rm IV})
, from (
1.2
), (
4.67
) and
\bm{k}\notin Q_{1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{z}\in X}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|\leq\delta_{0}^{-2}\delta_{1}^{-2}\sum_{\bm{z}\in X}e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{y}\|)}.

(4.96)

By
\bm{z}\in X
,
\bm{y}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{x})
(
\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{1}}{3}\geq 2\|\bm{x}-\bm{z}\|
) and (
3.2
), we get

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{z}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|-\|\bm{x}-\bm{z}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-2\rho\frac{\|\bm{x}-\bm{z}\|}{(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|)\log(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|)}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{1}{\log N_{1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|).

(4.97)

Hence, combining (
4.96
), (
4.97
),
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{1}}{3}\gg 1
, (
4.3
) and
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho
gives

\displaystyle({\rm IV})=

\displaystyle\ \ \sum_{\bm{z}\in X}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \delta_{0}^{-2}\delta_{1}^{-2}\cdot e^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|)}\cdot\sum_{\bm{z}\in X}e^{-\frac{1}{10}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{y}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \delta_{0}^{-2}\delta_{1}^{-2}\cdot D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)\cdot e^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \frac{1}{2}e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)},

(4.98)

where
D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)
is defined in (
2.1
). From (
4.94
), (
4.95
) and (
4.98
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}.

(4.99)

Case 3
:

\bm{x}\in\Lambda
and
\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})
. From (
4.2
), we get

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|=0\leq e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}.

(4.100)

In summary, we obtain

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\text{ for all $\bm{x},\bm{y}\in\Lambda$}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq D\left(\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\right)<+\infty,

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq D\left(\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\right)<+\infty,

where
D\left(\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\right)
is defined in (
2.1
). By Schur’s test, we can get

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}\|\leq D\left(\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{0}\right)\leq D\left(\frac{5}{16}\alpha\right)<+\infty.

From
\varepsilon\ll 1
, we have
\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\varepsilon\mathcal{K}
is invertible and

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\varepsilon\mathcal{K})^{-1}\|\leq 2.

(4.101)

At this time,

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}=(\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\varepsilon\mathcal{K})^{-1}\mathcal{L}

(4.102)

exists. Then, we estimate
\|\mathcal{L}\|
in order to estimate
\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|
by using similar methods.

We deal with
\sup_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|
first. We divide the discussion into two cases:

Case 1
:

\bm{x}\in\Lambda\setminus\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
. From (
4.26
) and (
4.2
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq(\#O(\bm{x}))\cdot\|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle<(N_{1}+1)^{d}\cdot 2\kappa_{1}^{-1}\delta_{0}^{-2}<\delta_{0}^{-\frac{5}{2}}.

(4.103)

Case 2
:

\bm{x}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
for some
\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}\setminus Q_{1}
. In this case, by (
4.69
) and (
4.2
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq(\#(O(\bm{x})))\cdot\|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle<\delta_{0}^{-3}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

(4.104)

Since
\theta,\theta_{1}\in\mathbb{D}_{R}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{1}\|\leq\sqrt{4R^{2}+\frac{1}{4}}

and

\displaystyle\delta_{0}^{-3}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}>\delta_{0}^{-\frac{5}{2}}.

(4.105)

Combining (
4.103
)–(
4.105
), we have

\displaystyle\ \ \sup_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \delta_{0}^{-3}\sup_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right).

(4.106)

Now, we estimate
\sup_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|
. We again divide the discussion into two cases:

Case 1
:

\bm{y}\in\Lambda\setminus\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}
. In this case,

\displaystyle\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})\text{ iff }\bm{x}\in\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y}).

At this time, if
\bm{x}\in\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})
, then
\bm{x}\in\Lambda\setminus\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
(
O(\bm{x})=\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{x})
is
0
-good). Hence by (
4.3
), (
4.26
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq(\#(\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})))\cdot 2\kappa_{1}^{-1}\delta_{0}^{-2}<\delta_{0}^{-\frac{5}{2}}.

(4.107)

Case 2
:

\bm{y}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}
for some
\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}\setminus Q_{1}
. In this case,

\displaystyle\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})\text{ iff }\bm{x}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}\bigcup\left(\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})\right).

Therefore, from (
4.3
), (
4.26
) and (
4.69
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\bm{x}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|+\sum_{\bm{x}\in\left(\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})\right)\setminus(2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1})}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq(\#(2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}))\delta_{0}^{-2}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\ \ +(\#(\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})))\cdot 2\kappa_{1}^{-1}\delta_{0}^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{0}^{-3}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

(4.108)

Combining (
4.105
), (
4.107
) and (
4.108
) gives

\displaystyle\ \ \sup_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \delta_{0}^{-3}\sup_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right).

(4.109)

Then by (
4.106
), (
4.109
) and Schur’s test, we know

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}\|\leq\delta_{0}^{-3}\sup_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right).

(4.110)

Finally, from (
4.2
), (
4.101
), (
4.102
) and (
4.110
), we prove

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|\leq 2\delta_{0}^{-3}\sup_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right)<\delta_{1}^{-3}.

This completes the proof of (
4.82
).

We begin the proof of the off-diagonal decay estimate in (
4.83
). The argument requires the following lemma:

Lemma 4.7
.

Assuming
\bm{u}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
for some
\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}\setminus Q_{1}
and
\bm{v}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{u})=\Lambda\setminus\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}
, then there exists some
\bm{u}^{\prime}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{u})
such that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{u},\bm{v})|\leq e^{-\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{0}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{u}-\bm{u}^{\prime}\|)}\cdot|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{u}^{\prime},\bm{v})|,

(4.111)

where
\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{0}=\frac{3}{4}\alpha\left(1-\frac{30\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}\right)
.

Proof.

For a detailed proof, we refer to the Appendix
A
.
∎

In the following, we always assume that
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq 10\tilde{\zeta}_{1}
. The proof of (
4.83
) proceeds via an iterative application of (
4.34
) and (
4.111
). For
\bm{z}\in\Lambda
, from Corollary
4.4
and Lemma
4.7
, there exists
\bm{z}^{\prime}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{z})
(cf. (
4.2
)) such that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|\leq e^{-\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{0}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{z}^{\prime}\|)}\cdot|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{z}^{\prime},\bm{y})|,

(4.112)

where
\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{0}=\frac{3}{4}\alpha\left(1-\frac{30\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}\right)
.

Next, iterating (
4.112
) for
m\geq 2
steps leads to the following: there exist
\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{2},\cdots,\bm{x}_{m}\in\Lambda
such that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq e^{-\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{0}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|)\right)}|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{x}_{m},\bm{y})|,

(4.113)

where
\bm{x}_{0}=\bm{x}
and
\bm{x}_{k+1}=\bm{x}^{\prime}
,
k=0,1,\cdots,m-1
. We define
n\geq 1
to be the smallest integer so that
\bm{x}_{n}\in O(\bm{y})
(cf. (
4.2
)). We then have
\bm{x}_{i}\notin O(\bm{y})
for
i=0,1,\cdots,n-1
. We divide the discussion into two cases:

Case 1
:

n\leq\frac{\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)+3\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log(N_{1}+1)}{\frac{\alpha}{2}\log^{\rho}\left(\frac{N_{1}}{2}+1\right)}
. Using Lemma
3.1
and the triangle inequality implies

\displaystyle\ \ \sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}\left(1+\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|\right)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}n

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{n}-\bm{x}\|)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}n.

(4.114)

Since
\bm{x}_{n}\in O(\bm{y})
and
{\rm diam}(O(\bm{y}))\leq\tilde{\zeta}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{x}-\bm{x}_{n}\|\geq\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\|\bm{x}_{n}-\bm{y}\|\lambda\geq\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\tilde{\zeta}_{1}.

By
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq 10\tilde{\zeta}_{1}\gg N_{1}\gg 1
and
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho<\rho^{\prime}+1
, applying Lemma
3.2
with
x=\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|
and
y=\tilde{\zeta}_{1}
gives

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{n}-\bm{x}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\tilde{\zeta}_{1})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-2\rho\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{1}}{(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)\log(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|).

(4.115)

Under the same conditions, we have

\displaystyle C(\rho)\log^{\rho}n

\displaystyle\leq C(\rho)\log^{\rho}(2\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|))\leq\log(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}.

(4.116)

Combining (
4.114
), (
4.115
) and (
4.116
) shows

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|)\geq\left(1-\frac{2\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|).

(4.117)

From
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq 10\tilde{\zeta}_{1}\gg N_{1}\gg 1
, (
4.3
), (
4.82
), (
4.113
) and (
4.117
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{0}\left(1-\frac{2\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\delta_{1}^{-3}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)},

where
\alpha_{1}=\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{0}-\frac{15\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}=\frac{3}{4}\alpha\left(1-\frac{50\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}\right)
.

Case 2
:

n>\frac{\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)+3\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log(N_{1}+1)}{\frac{\alpha}{2}\log^{\rho}\left(\frac{N_{1}}{2}+1\right)}
. In this case, from (
4.82
), (
4.113
),
\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|\geq\frac{N_{1}}{2}
(since
\bm{x}_{k+1}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{x}_{k})
),
k=0,1\cdots,n-1
and

\displaystyle\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{0}=\frac{3}{4}\alpha\left(1-\frac{30\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}\right)>\frac{\alpha}{2},

it follows that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{0}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|)\right)}|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{x}_{n},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-n\cdot\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{0}\log^{\rho}\left(\frac{N_{1}}{2}+1\right)}e^{3\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log^{\rho}(N_{1}+1)}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\leq e^{-\alpha_{1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}.

This concludes the proof of Lemma
4.6
.
∎

4.3.
The proof of Theorem
4.2
: (from
\mathscr{P}_{s}
to
\mathscr{P}_{s+1}
)

We have completed the proof of
\mathscr{P}_{1}
in Subsection
4.2
. Now, assuming the validity of
\mathscr{P}_{t}

(1\leq t\leq s)
, the proof of Theorem
4.2
reduces to establishing.

Recall that

\displaystyle Q_{s}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=\left\{\bm{k}\in P_{s}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}\right\},\ Q_{s}=Q_{s}^{+}\cup Q_{s}^{-},

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{s}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=\left\{\bm{k}\in P_{s}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}\right\},\ \tilde{Q}_{s}=\tilde{Q}_{s}^{+}\cup\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-}.

We distinguish the verification into three steps.

Step 1

:
Estimates of
\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|
.

In this step, we apply the resolvent indentity and Rouché’s theorem to construct
\theta_{s+1}=\theta_{s+1}(E)
such that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle<\delta_{s}^{-2}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

(4.118)

We again divide the discussion into two cases.

Case 1

: The case
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
occurs, i.e.,

\displaystyle{\rm dist}(\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-},Q_{s}^{+})>100N_{s+1}^{10}.

(4.119)

Remark 4.5
.

We can prove similar to Remark
4.4
that

\displaystyle{\rm dist}(\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-},Q_{s}^{+})={\rm dist}(\tilde{Q}_{s}^{+},Q_{s}^{-}).

Thus (
4.119
) also implies that

\displaystyle{\rm dist}(\tilde{Q}_{s}^{+},Q_{s}^{-})>100N_{s+1}^{10}.

(4.120)

By (
4.25
) and the definitions of
Q_{s}^{\pm}
and
\tilde{Q}_{s}^{\pm}
, we obtain

\displaystyle Q_{s}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}\right\},

(4.121)

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{s}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}\right\}.

Assuming (
4.119
) holds true, we define

\displaystyle P_{s+1}=Q_{s},\ \bm{l}_{s}=\bm{0}.

(4.122)

By (
4.121
), we have

\displaystyle P_{s+1}\subset\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \min_{\sigma=\pm 1}(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\sigma\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}})<\delta_{s}\right\},

(4.123)

which proves (
4.6
) in the case
(\bm{C}1)_{s+1}
. Thus from (
4.120
), we obtain for
\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s+1}
with
\bm{k}\neq\bm{k}^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\|\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime}\|\geq\min\left(100N_{s+1}^{10},\left(\frac{\gamma}{2\delta_{s}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tau}}\right)\geq 100N_{s+1}^{10}.

(4.124)

In the following, we associate each
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
with blocks
\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
,
2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
so that

\displaystyle\Lambda_{N_{s+1}}(\bm{k})\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda_{N_{s+1}+50N_{s}^{100}}(\bm{k}),

\displaystyle\Lambda_{2N_{s+1}}(\bm{k})\subset 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda_{2N_{s+1}+50N_{s}^{100}}(\bm{k}),

\displaystyle\Lambda_{N_{s+1}^{10}}(\bm{k})\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda_{N_{s+1}^{10}+50N_{s}^{100}}(\bm{k}),

and

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\ (s^{\prime}<s+1)\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\\
2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\ (s^{\prime}<s+1)\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\\
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\ (s^{\prime}<s+1)\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\\
{\rm dist}(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s+1})>10\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\ \text{for}\ \bm{k}\neq\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s+1}.\end{array}\right.

Moreover, the translated set of
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}

(4.129)

is both independent of
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
and symmetric about the origin. For full proofs of (
4.3
) and (
4.129
), we refer to page 23 of
[
CSZ24b
]
. In summary, we have now established
(\bm{a})_{s+1}
and
(\bm{b})_{s+1}
in the case
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
.

Now we turn to the proof of
(\bm{c})_{s+1}
. For any
\bm{k}^{\prime}\in Q_{s}\ (=P_{s+1})
, observe that

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s+1}.

For each
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
, define

\displaystyle A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}=A_{\bm{k}}^{s}.

By construction,

\displaystyle A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s}\text{ and }\#A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}=\#A_{\bm{k}}^{s}\leq 2^{s}.

It remains to show that
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
s
-
{\rm good}
and the set
A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}
is independent of
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
and symmetrical about the origin. Detailed arguments for these claims appear on page 26 of
[
CSZ24b
]
. This completes the proof of
(\bm{c})_{s+1}
in the case
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
.

We now proceed to establish properties
(\bm{d})_{s+1}
and
(\bm{f})_{s+1}
under the assumption of case
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
. For
\bm{k}\in Q_{s}^{-}
, we consider

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z):=(\mathcal{T}(z))_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}=((v(z+\bm{n}\cdot\bm{\omega})-E)\delta_{\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{W})_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}

defined in

\displaystyle\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ |z-\theta_{s}|\leq\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}\}.

(4.130)

If
\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})
, then
0\neq\|\bm{k}^{\prime}-\bm{k}\|\leq 3N_{s+1}^{10}
. Thus from (
4.3
),

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|(\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime})\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\gamma}{(3N_{s+1}^{10})^{\tau}}-\delta_{s}>\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{4}}}.

By (
4.120
), we have
\bm{k}^{\prime}\notin\tilde{Q}_{s}^{+}
, and thus

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}>\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}.

From
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
s
-
{\rm good}
(cf.
(\bm{c})_{s+1}
) and (
4.23
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle<2\delta_{s-1}^{-3}\times\sup\limits_{\{\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})\}}(\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1})

\displaystyle<\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{-2\times\frac{1}{100}}.

(4.131)

One may restate as

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle<\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{-2\times\frac{1}{100}}.

Notice that

\displaystyle\|z-(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\leq|z-\theta_{s}|+\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}+\delta_{s}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}.

(4.132)

Thus by Neumann series argument, we can show

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle<\delta_{s}^{-2\times\frac{1}{100}}.

(4.133)

We now apply the Schur complement lemma to derive the required estimates. Lemma (
A.1
) asserts that the inverse
(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))^{-1}
is governed by the Schur complement associated with
((\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k})
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}-\left(\vphantom{\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right.

\displaystyle\ \ \left.\times\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}\right).

Our primary objective is to analyze
\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)
. Observing that

\displaystyle A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}=A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k},

we decompose the restriction operator as follows

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}+\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}=

\displaystyle\ \ \mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle\ \ +\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}.

Then

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}+\mathcal{E}_{s}(z)-\left(\vphantom{\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}\right.

\displaystyle\ \ \left.\times\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\right),

(4.134)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{s}(z)

\displaystyle=\left(\vphantom{\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}\right.

\displaystyle\ \ \ \left.\times\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ +\left(\vphantom{\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}\right.

\displaystyle\ \ \ \left.\times\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\right).

From
{\rm dist}(A_{\bm{k}}^{s},\partial\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s})>\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}
, (
1.2
), (
4.3
), (
2.1
) and (
4.133
), for
\bm{x},\bm{y}\in A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}
, we get

\displaystyle|\mathcal{E}_{s}(z)(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq 2D\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)D(\alpha)\delta_{s}^{-\frac{1}{50}}e^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{2}\right)}\leq\delta_{s}^{20}.

(4.135)

Since
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s}
is
(s-1)
-
{\rm good}
(cf.
(\bm{c})_{s}
), (
4.23
) and
(
4.24
)
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s}}^{-1}\|\leq\delta_{s-1}^{-3}.

Equivalently,

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|\leq\delta_{s-1}^{-3}.

(4.136)

Within the domain specified by (
4.130
), we claim the following estimates:

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta_{s-1}^{-3},

(4.137)

\displaystyle|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}})^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{s-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\ \text{for $\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|>10\tilde{\zeta}_{s-1}$.}

(4.138)

Proof of the Claim.

We proceed via the following steps:

Step 1

:
Operator Decomposition
. Define the comparison operators:

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{1}=(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}},\ \mathcal{T}_{2}=(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}.

The difference
\mathcal{D}_{1}=\mathcal{T}_{1}-\mathcal{T}_{2}
is diagonal, with norm bounded by

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{D}_{1}\|\leq 2\kappa_{2}\sqrt{4R^{2}+\frac{1}{4}}\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}

via (
1.3
) and (
4.3
).

Step 2

:
Neumann Series Argument
.
Applying the Neumann expansion yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{2}^{-1}=(\mathcal{I}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}-\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}\mathcal{D}_{1})^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}=\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}\mathcal{D}_{1})^{i}\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}.

The norm estimate follows by combining (
4.136
) with the Neumann series expansion. Specifically, from (
4.136
) we have the a priori bound
\|\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}\|\leq\delta_{s-1}^{-3}
, which when combined with the smallness condition on
\|\mathcal{D}_{1}\|
yields

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{-1}\|\leq\left(\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\|\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}\|^{i}\cdot\|\mathcal{D}_{1}\|^{i}\right)\|\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}\|\leq 2\delta_{s-1}^{-3}.

Step 3

:
Generalization to Subdomains
.
For any subdomain
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{t}\subset(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})
with
0\leq t\leq s-1
and
\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{t}\setminus Q_{t}
, we similarly obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{t}}^{-1}(z)\|\leq 2\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{t}}^{-1}\|\leq 2\delta_{t}^{-3}.

Step 4

:
Off-Diagonal Decay
.
The decay estimate (
4.138
) follows by iterating the resolvent identity, following the same methodology used to establish
(\bm{e})_{s-1}
. We omit the repetitive details.

We complete proof of the claim.
∎

To estimate the difference between
\mathcal{S}_{s}
and
\mathcal{S}_{s+1}
, we employ the resolvent identity combined with the decay properties of
\mathcal{W}
. For clarity, we first define the following sets:
X=(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}
,
Z_{1}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{4}}\cap X
,
Z_{2}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}}\cap X
and
Y=(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}
. For any
\bm{m}\in X
and
\bm{n}\in Y
, the resolvent identity yields

\displaystyle((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\sum_{\bm{l}\in X\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n}).

If
\bm{m}\in Z_{2}
, since (
1.2
), (
3.1
), (
2.1
), (
4.133
), (
4.137
), (
4.138
),
{\rm dist}(Z_{1},Y\setminus X)\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}\gg 10\tilde{\zeta}_{s-1}
and
{\rm dist}(Z_{2},X\setminus Z_{1})\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}\gg 10\tilde{\zeta}_{s-1}
, we can get

\displaystyle({\rm I})=

\displaystyle\ |((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \sum_{\bm{l}\in Z_{1}\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\ \ +\sum_{\bm{l}\in X\setminus Z_{1}\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 2\delta_{s-1}^{-3}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{1}{50}}e^{-\frac{9}{10}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}\right)}(\#(Z_{1}))D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ +\delta_{s}^{-\frac{1}{50}}e^{-\alpha_{s-1}\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}\right)+\alpha_{s-1}C(\rho)\log 2}(\#(X\setminus Z_{1}))D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\ \delta_{s}^{20}.

If
\bm{m}\in X\setminus Z_{2}
, by (
1.2
), (
2.1
), (
4.133
) and (
4.137
), we obtain

\displaystyle({\rm II})=

\displaystyle\ |((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \sum_{\bm{l}\in X\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 2\delta_{s-1}^{-3}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{1}{50}}(\#X)D(\alpha).

For
\bm{i}\in A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}
,
\bm{n}\in Y
, since (
1.2
), (
2.1
),
{\rm dist}(A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k},X\setminus Z_{2})\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{16}\gg 10\tilde{\zeta}_{s-1}
and (
4.133
)–(
4.138
), we have

\displaystyle\ \ |(\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1})(\bm{i},\bm{n})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -(\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X})(\bm{i},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \sum_{\bm{m}\in Z_{2}}|\mathcal{W}(\bm{i},\bm{m})|\cdot({\rm I})+\sum_{\bm{m}\in X\setminus Z_{2}}|\mathcal{W}(\bm{i},\bm{m})|\cdot({\rm II})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ D(\alpha)\delta_{s}^{20}+2\delta_{s-1}^{-3}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{1}{50}}(\#X)D(\alpha)D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)e^{-\frac{9}{10}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{16}\right)}<\delta_{s}^{15}.

It then follows that

\displaystyle\ \ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}+O(\delta_{s}^{15}).

(4.139)

From (
4.134
), (
4.135
) and (
4.139
), we have

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}+O(\delta_{s}^{15})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}\mathcal{M}_{s}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}+O(\delta_{s}^{15}).

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{M}_{s}(z)_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}-\left(\vphantom{(\mathcal{M}_{s}(z))_{X})^{-1}}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s}(z))_{X})^{-1}\right.

\displaystyle\ \left.\times\ \mathcal{R}_{X}\mathcal{M}_{s}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\right)+O(\delta_{s}^{15})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{S}_{s}(z)+O(\delta_{s}^{15}),

which implies (
4.16
) for the
(s+1)
-th step. Building upon the estimates from (
4.15
), (
4.17
) and (
4.130
), we first note the lower bound

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s}(z)|\geq\delta_{s-1}\|z-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\|z+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

Applying Lemma
A.3
with the cardinality bound
\#(A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k})\leq 2^{s}
and using (
4.16
), we derive

\displaystyle\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=\det\mathcal{S}_{s}(z)+O((2^{s})^{2}(4|v|_{R})^{2^{s}}\delta_{s}^{15})

\displaystyle=\det\mathcal{S}_{s}(z)+O(\delta_{s}^{10}).

For the torus norm, we establish

\displaystyle\|z+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\|z-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle>\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}-\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}-\delta_{s}

\displaystyle>\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}.

This leads to the improved estimate

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)|\geq\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}|z-\theta_{s}+r_{s+1}(z)|,

where
r_{s+1}(z)
is analytic in the region (
4.130
) with
|r_{s+1}(z)|<\delta_{s}^{8}
. Finally, the equation

\displaystyle(z-\theta_{s})+r_{s+1}(z)=0

has a unique root
\theta_{s+1}
in (
4.130
) satisfying

\displaystyle|\theta_{s+1}-\theta_{s}|=|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|<\delta_{s}^{8}.

(4.140)

For
|z|=\delta_{s}^{1/10}
, we obtain (since
|r_{1}(z)|<\delta_{s}^{8}
and (
4.140
))

\displaystyle\frac{|r_{s+1}(\theta_{1})-r_{s+1}(z)|}{|z-\theta_{s}+r_{s+1}(z)|}\leq 4\delta_{s}^{7},

which implies

\displaystyle\frac{|z-\theta_{s+1}|}{|z-\theta_{s}+r_{s+1}(z)|}=\frac{|z-\theta_{s}+r_{s+1}(\theta_{1})|}{|z-\theta_{s}+r_{s+1}(z)|}\in[1-4\delta_{s}^{7},1+4\delta_{s}^{7}].

By the maximum modulus principle, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\leq\frac{|z-\theta_{s+1}|}{|z-\theta_{s}+r_{s+1}(z)|}\leq 2.

Moreover, the root
\theta_{s+1}
is unique for
\det\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)=0
in (
4.130
). Since
\|z+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}>\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}
and
|\theta_{s+1}-\theta_{s}|<\delta_{s}^{8}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|z+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\leq\|z+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\leq 2\|z+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

Thus, for
z
in (
4.130
), we establish the key determinant bound

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)|\geq\delta_{s}\|z-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\|z+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

(4.141)

Since
\delta_{s+1}=\delta_{s}^{10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}
, we obtain
\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{10^{4}}}\ll\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}
. Recalling (
4.130
) and (
4.140
), the estimate (
4.141
) remains valid for

\displaystyle\|z-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{10^{4}}}.

For
\bm{k}\in Q_{s}^{+}
, we analyze
\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)
in the region

\displaystyle\left\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ |z+\theta_{s}|\leq\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}\right\}.

(4.142)

An analogous argument demonstrates that the equation
\det\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)=0
possesses a unique root
\theta_{s+1}^{{}^{\prime}}
within the region defined by (
4.142
). We now establish the antisymmetry property
\theta_{s+1}+\theta_{s+1}^{\prime}=0
. In fact, Lemma
A.2
guarantees that
\det\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)
is an even function of
z
. The uniqueness of roots in (
4.142
) immediately implies
\theta_{s+1}^{\prime}=-\theta_{s+1}
. Consequently, for all
z
in (
4.142
), the determinant bound (
4.141
) remains valid. This establishes the estimate throughout the symmetric domain

\displaystyle\left\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\min_{\sigma=\pm 1}\|z+\sigma\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{10^{4}}}\right\},

thereby completing the proof of (
4.17
) for the
(s+1)
-th step. Combining (
4.121
)–(
4.122
) and the following

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{100}},\ |\theta_{s+1}-\theta_{s}|<\delta_{s}^{8}\Rightarrow\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s},

we get

\displaystyle\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \min_{\sigma=\pm 1}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\sigma\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{100}}\right\}\subset P_{s+1},

which proves (
4.25
) at the
(s+1)
-th step. Finally, we want to estimate
\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|
. For
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
, by (
4.123
), we obtain

\displaystyle\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\in\left\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ \min_{\sigma=\pm 1}\|z+\sigma\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}\right\},

which together with (
4.141
) implies

\displaystyle\ |\det(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ |\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \delta_{s}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

By (
4.16
), Cramer’s rule and Lemma
3.3
, one has

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ |\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})|^{-1}

\displaystyle\ \ \cdot\|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{*}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 2^{s}\cdot(4|v|_{R})^{2^{s}}\delta_{s}^{-1}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

From Lemma
A.1
and (
4.131
), we get

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle<4(1+\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\|)^{2}

\displaystyle\ \ \times(1+\|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{-1}\|)

\displaystyle<\delta_{s}^{-2}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|^{-1}_{\mathbb{T}}.

(4.143)

Case 2.

The case
(\bm{C}2)_{s}
occurs, i.e.,

\displaystyle{\rm dist}(\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-},Q_{s}^{+})\leq 100N_{s+1}^{10}.

Then there exist
\bm{i}_{s}\in Q_{s}^{+}
and
\bm{j}_{s}\in\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-}
with
\|\bm{i}_{s}-\bm{j}_{s}\|\leq 100N_{s+1}^{10}
such that

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{i}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s},\ \|\theta+\bm{j}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}.

Define

\displaystyle\bm{l}_{s}=\bm{i}_{s}-\bm{j}_{s}.

Using (
4.6
) and (
4
) yields

\displaystyle Q_{s}^{+},\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-}\subset P_{s}\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}.

Thus
\bm{i}_{s}\equiv\bm{j}_{s}\ (\text{mod}\ \mathbb{Z}^{d})
and
\bm{l}_{s}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}
. Define

\displaystyle O_{s+1}=Q_{s}^{-}\cup(Q_{s}^{+}-\bm{l}_{s}).

(4.144)

For every
\bm{o}\in O_{s+1}
, define its mirror point as

\displaystyle\bm{o}^{*}=\bm{o}+\bm{l}_{s}.

Then we have

\displaystyle O_{s+1}\subset\left\{\bm{o}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{o}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}\right\}

and

\displaystyle O_{s+1}+\bm{l}_{s}\subset\left\{\bm{o}^{*}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{o}^{*}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}\right\}.

Then by (
4.25
), we obtain

\displaystyle O_{s+1}\cup(O_{s+1}+\bm{l}_{s})\subset P_{s}.

(4.145)

Next, define

\displaystyle P_{s+1}=\left\{\frac{1}{2}(\bm{o}+\bm{o}^{*}):\ \bm{o}\in O_{s+1}\right\}=\left\{\bm{o}+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}:\ \bm{o}\in O_{s+1}\right\}.

(4.146)

Notice that

\displaystyle\min

\displaystyle\left(\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}},\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}-\frac{1}{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{l}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}+2\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(\|\theta+\bm{i}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}+\|\theta+\bm{j}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}})<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}.

Since
\delta_{s}\ll 1
, only one of

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{100}}\ \text{and}\ \left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}-\frac{1}{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{100}}

occurs. First, we consider the case of

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}.

(4.147)

Let
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
. Since
\bm{k}=\bm{o}+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}
(for some
\bm{o}\in O_{s+1}
), we have

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\leq\|\theta+\bm{o}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}+\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<3\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}},

(4.148)

which implies

\displaystyle P_{s+1}\subset\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<3\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}\right\}.

(4.149)

Moreover, if
\bm{k}\neq\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s+1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime}\|\geq\left(\frac{\gamma}{6\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tau}}\gg 100N_{s+1}^{100}.

Similar to the proof that appears in
Case 1
(i.e., the
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
case), we can associate each
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
with the blocks
\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
,
2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
satisfying

\displaystyle\Lambda_{100N_{s+1}^{10}}(\bm{k})\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda_{100N_{s+1}^{10}+50N_{s}^{100}}(\bm{k}),

\displaystyle\Lambda_{200N_{s+1}^{10}}(\bm{k})\subset 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda_{200N_{s+1}^{10}+50N_{s}^{100}}(\bm{k}),

\displaystyle\Lambda_{N_{s+1}^{100}}(\bm{k})\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda_{N_{s+1}^{100}+50N_{s}^{100}}(\bm{k})

and

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\ (s^{\prime}<s+1)\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\\
2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\ (s^{\prime}<s+1)\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\\
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\ (s^{\prime}<s+1)\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\\
{\rm dist}(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s+1})>10{\rm diam}\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\ \text{for}\ \bm{k}\neq\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s+1}.\end{array}\right.

Furthermore, the translated domain

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}

is independent of
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
and symmetrical about the origin. This completes the verification of properties
(\bm{a})_{s+1}
and
(\bm{b})_{s+1}
for case
(\bm{C}2)_{s}
.

For each
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
, let
\bm{o},\bm{o}^{*}\in P_{s}
be the unique pair specified by (
4.145
). We define

\displaystyle A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}=A_{\bm{o}}^{s}\cup A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s},

where
\bm{o}\in O_{s+1}
and
\bm{k}=\frac{1}{2}(\bm{o}+\bm{o}^{*})
per (
4.146
). This construction satisfies:

\displaystyle A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}

\displaystyle\subset\Omega_{\bm{o}}^{s}\cup\Omega_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},

\displaystyle\#A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}

\displaystyle=\#A_{\bm{o}}^{s}+\#A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}\leq 2^{s+1}.

The proof that
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
s

appears on page 32 of

, which concludes the verification of

for case

.

We analyze the operator

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z):=\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}(z)=((v(z+\bm{n}\cdot\bm{\omega})-E)\delta_{\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{W})_{\bm{n}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}

defined on the domain

\displaystyle\left\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ |z|\leq\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}\right\}.

(4.154)

If
\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})
, then
\bm{k}^{\prime}\neq\bm{o},\bm{o}^{*}
and
\|\bm{k}^{\prime}-\bm{o}\|,\|\bm{k}^{\prime}-\bm{o}^{*}\|\leq 4N_{s+1}^{100}
. Thus

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|(\bm{k}^{\prime}-\bm{o})\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\|\theta+\bm{o}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\gamma}{(4N_{s+1}^{100})^{\tau}}-2\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}>\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{4}}}

and

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|(\bm{k}^{\prime}-\bm{o}^{*})\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\|\theta+\bm{o}^{*}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\gamma}{(4N_{s+1}^{100})^{\tau}}-2\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}>\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{4}}}.

By (
4.23
) and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
s
-
{\rm good}
(cf.
(\bm{c})_{s+1}
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta_{s}^{-3}\times\sup\limits_{\{\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})\}}(\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1})

\displaystyle<\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{-3\times\frac{1}{10^{4}}}.

(4.155)

One may restate (
4.155
) as

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|<\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{-3\times\frac{1}{10^{4}}}.

From the estimate

\displaystyle\|z-(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\leq|z|+\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}+3\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}},

(4.156)

we apply a Neumann series argument to obtain the key inverse bound

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|<\delta_{s}^{-3\times\frac{1}{10^{4}}}.

(4.157)

By Lemma
A.1
, the inverse
(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))^{-1}
is governed by the Schur complement associated with
((\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k})
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}-\left(\vphantom{\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right.

\displaystyle\ \ \left.\times\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}\right).

We now turn to the analysis of
\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)
. Since

\displaystyle A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}

\displaystyle=(A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k})\cup(A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}),

\displaystyle A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}

\displaystyle\subset\Omega_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k},\ A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}\subset\Omega_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}

and

\displaystyle{\rm dist}(\Omega_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k},\Omega_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k})>10\tilde{\zeta}_{s},

we have

\displaystyle(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}=((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}})\oplus((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}).

From
{\rm dist}(A_{\bm{o}}^{s},\partial\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}}^{s})>\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}
and
{\rm dist}(A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s},\partial\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s})>\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{o}}^{s})-\bm{k}}+O(\delta_{s}^{20}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{o}}^{s})-\bm{k}}+O(\delta_{s}^{20}).

For notational clarity, we define the following sets:

\displaystyle X=(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{o}}^{s})-\bm{k},\ X^{*}=(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s})-k,\ Y=(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k},

\displaystyle Z_{1}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{4}}\cap X,\ Z_{2}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}}\cap X,\ Z_{1}^{*}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{4}}\cap X^{*},\ Z_{2}^{*}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}}\cap X^{*}.

The Schur complement decomposes as

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)=

\displaystyle\ ((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}})\oplus((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}})

\displaystyle-\left(\vphantom{\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}}(\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\oplus\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}*}^{s}-\bm{k}})\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right.

\displaystyle\ \times\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\left.\vphantom{\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\right)^{-1}}\times\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)(\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\oplus\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}*}^{s}-\bm{k}})\right).

Equivalently, for the translated operator,

From (

(4.158)

\displaystyle|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{s-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\ \text{for $\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|>10\tilde{\zeta}_{s-1}$.}

(4.159)

For
\bm{m}\in X
and
\bm{n}\in Y
, the resolvent identity gives

\displaystyle((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\sum_{\bm{l}\in X\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n}).

\displaystyle\ |((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \sum_{\bm{l}\in Z_{1}\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\ \ +\sum_{\bm{l}\in X\setminus Z_{1}\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 2\delta_{s-1}^{-3}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{3}{10^{4}}}e^{-\frac{9}{10}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}\right)}(\#(Z_{1}))D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ +\delta_{s}^{-\frac{3}{10^{4}}}e^{-\alpha_{s-1}\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}\right)+\alpha_{s-1}C(\rho)\log 2}(\#(X\setminus Z_{1}))D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)

\displaystyle\ \delta_{s}^{20}.

\displaystyle\ \sum_{\bm{l}\in X\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\ 2\delta_{s-1}^{-3}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{3}{10^{4}}}(\#X)D(\alpha).

,

\displaystyle\ \ -\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}(\bm{i},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \sum_{\bm{m}\in Z_{2}}|\mathcal{W}(\bm{i},\bm{m})|\cdot({\rm I})+\sum_{\bm{m}\in X\setminus Z_{2}}|\mathcal{W}(\bm{i},\bm{m})|\cdot({\rm II})

It then follows that

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}+O(\delta_{s}^{15}).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X^{*}}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X^{*}}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X^{*}})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X^{*}}+O(\delta_{s}^{15}).

As a result,

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)=\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\oplus\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)+O(\delta_{s}^{15}).

From (
4.147
) and (
4.154
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}\leq|z|+\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}+\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}

and

\displaystyle\left\|z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}|\leq|z|+\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}+\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}.

Consequently, both translated points
z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}
and

lie within the domain defined by (
4.15
). Combining this with the determinant estimate (
4.17
) yields

\displaystyle\left|\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\geq\delta_{s-1}\left\|\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)-\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\left\|\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}},

(4.160)

\displaystyle\left|\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\geq\delta_{s-1}\left\|\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)-\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\left\|\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

(4.161)

Moreover, since
\#(A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k})\leq 2^{s+1}
, (
4.16
) and Lemma
A.3
, we have

\displaystyle\ \ \sup_{\bm{x}\in A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}\sum_{\bm{y}\in A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}|\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \sup_{\bm{x}\in A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}\sum_{\bm{y}\in A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}\left|\left(\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\oplus\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\right)(\bm{x},\bm{y})\right|+O(\delta_{s}^{10})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ 2|v|_{R}+\sum_{i=0}^{s}\delta_{s}<4|v|_{R},

(4.162)

and

\displaystyle\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\cdot\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)+O((2^{s+1})^{2}(4|v|_{R})^{2^{s+1}}\delta_{s}^{15})

\displaystyle=\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\cdot\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)+O(\delta_{s}^{10}).

(4.163)

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\|z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|\bm{l}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\left\|z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{0}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle>\frac{\gamma}{(100N_{s+1}^{10})^{\tau}}-2\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}>\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{4}}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|\bm{l}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\left\|z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{0}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle>\frac{\gamma}{(100N_{s+1}^{10})^{\tau}}-2\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}>\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{4}}}.

(4.165)

Let
z_{s+1}
satisfy

\displaystyle z_{s+1}\equiv\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\ (\text{mod}\ \mathbb{Z}),\ |z_{s+1}|=\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}.

From estimates (
4.160
)–(
4.165
), we derive the determinant bound

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)|\geq\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}|(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(z)|,

where

is analytic in the domain (
4.154
) and satisfies

\displaystyle|r_{s+1}(z)|<\delta_{s}^{8}.

(4.167)

By the Rouché’s theorem, the equation

\displaystyle(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(z)=0

has exactly two roots
\theta_{s+1}
and
\theta_{s+1}^{{}^{\prime}}
in (

\pm z_{s+1}
. Notice that the zero sets coincide

Since
\det\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)
is even, we have

\displaystyle\theta_{s+1}^{{}^{\prime}}=-\theta_{s+1}.

Assuming both

\displaystyle|z_{s+1}-\theta_{s+1}|>|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|^{\frac{1}{2}}\ \text{and}\ |z_{s+1}-\theta_{s+1}|>|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|^{\frac{1}{2}}

leads to

\displaystyle|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|>|z_{s+1}-\theta_{s+1}|\cdot|z_{s+1}+\theta_{s+1}|>|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|,

a contradiction. Thus, without loss of generality,

\displaystyle|\theta_{s+1}-z_{s+1}|\leq|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|^{\frac{1}{2}}<\delta_{s}^{4}.

(4.168)

Moreover, for
|z|=\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}
, we have (since (

) and (
4.167
))

\displaystyle\frac{|r_{s+1}(z)-r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|}{|(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|}\leq 2\delta_{s}^{7}.

Combined with
\theta_{s+1}^{2}-z_{s+1}^{2}+r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})=0
, this yields

\displaystyle\ \frac{|(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(z)|}{|(z-\theta_{s+1})\cdot(z+\theta_{s+1})|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{|(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(z)|}{|(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|}

\displaystyle\in

\displaystyle\ \left[1-2\delta_{s}^{7},1+2\delta_{s}^{7}\right].

By the maximum modulus principle, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\leq\frac{|(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(z)|}{|(z-\theta_{s+1})\cdot(z+\theta_{s+1})|}\leq 2.

Thus for
z
in (
4.154
), we obtain

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)|\geq\delta_{s}\|z-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\|z+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

(4.169)

Since
\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{10^{4}}}<\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}
, (
4.166
) and (
4.168
), we have the containment

\displaystyle\left\{z\in\mathbb{C}\ :\ \min_{\sigma=\pm 1}|z+\sigma\theta_{s+1}|<\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{10^{4}}}\right\}\subset\left\{z\in\mathbb{C}\ :\ |z|\leq\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}\right\},

proveing (
4.17
) for step
(s+1)
. The inequalities

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{100}}\text{ and }|\theta_{s+1}-z_{s+1}|<\delta_{s}^{4}

imply

\displaystyle\left\|\theta+\left(\bm{k}+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\right)\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}.

For
\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}
satisfying

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{100}},

we have

\displaystyle\bm{k}+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}\ \text{and}\ \left\|\theta+\left(\bm{k}+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\right)\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}.

Therefore, by (
4.121
), we have

. Recalling (

) and (
