\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
. Thus

\displaystyle\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{100}}\right\}\subset P_{s+1}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{100}}\right\}\subset P_{s+1}.

This establishes (
4.25
) for the
(s+1)
-th step.

Finally, we estimate
\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|
. For
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
, by (
4.148
), we have

\displaystyle\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\in\left\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ \|z\|_{\mathbb{T}}\leq\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}\right\}.

Thus from (
4.169
), we obtain

\displaystyle\ |\det(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ |\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \delta_{s}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|.

By (
4.16
), Cramer’s rule and Lemma
3.3
, one has

\displaystyle\ \|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ |\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})|^{-1}

\displaystyle\cdot\|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{*}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 2^{s+1}\cdot(4|v|_{R})^{2^{s+1}}\delta_{s}^{-1}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

From Lemma
A.1
and reference (
4.155
), we derive the following
\ell^{2}
-norm estimate

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle<4(1+\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\|)^{2}

\displaystyle\ \ \times(1+\|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{-1}\|)

\displaystyle<\delta_{s}^{-2}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

(4.170)

For the case of

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}-\frac{1}{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}},

(4.171)

we obtain the inclusion

\displaystyle P_{s+1}\subset\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \left\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\frac{1}{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<3\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{100}}\right\}.

(4.172)

Now we examine
\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)
in the shifted neighborhood

\displaystyle\left\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ \left|z-\frac{1}{2}\right|\leq\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}\right\}.

(4.173)

A similar argument shows that
\det\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)=0
has two roots
\theta_{s+1}
and
1-\theta_{s+1}
in the set defined by (
4.173
) such that (
4.162
)–(
4.170
) hold true for
z
in (
4.173
). Hence, if (
4.171
) holds, then (
4.162
)–(
4.170
) hold true for
z
in

\displaystyle\left\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ \left\|z-\frac{1}{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}\leq\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10^{3}}}\right\}.

By (
4.172
), for any
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
, we also have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|<\delta_{s}^{-2}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

Therefore, we have established the desired estimates of
\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|

in both cases
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
and
(\bm{C}2)_{s}
.

Step2

:
Off-diagonal estimates of
\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}
.

The main result of this step is Theorem
4.8
. Recalling

\displaystyle\delta_{s+1}=\delta_{s}^{10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}

and

\displaystyle Q_{s+1}^{\pm}=\left\{\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s+1}\right\},\ Q_{s+1}=Q_{s+1}^{+}\cup Q_{s+1}^{-},

we have

Theorem 4.8
.

For
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}\setminus Q_{s+1}
, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|<e^{-\alpha^{\prime}_{s}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\ \text{for $\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta_{s}}}{10}$},

(4.174)

where
\alpha^{\prime}_{s}=\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{20\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)
is defined in (
4.22
).

To prove Theorem
4.8
, we first establish two preparatory lemmas regarding iterative estimates:

Lemma 4.9
.

Assume
t\leq s
and
\Lambda^{\prime}\subset\Lambda\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}
be finite sets. If
\Lambda^{\prime}\subset\Lambda
is
t
-good,
\bm{u}^{\prime}\in\Lambda^{\prime}
and
\bm{v}\in\Lambda\setminus\Lambda^{\prime}
, then there is some
\bm{u}^{\prime}\in\Lambda\setminus\Lambda^{\prime}
such that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{u},\bm{v})|\leq(\#\Lambda^{\prime})\cdot e^{-\alpha_{t}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{u}-\bm{u}^{\prime}\|)}\cdot|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{u}^{\prime},\bm{v})|\cdot\delta_{t}^{-3}.

(4.175)

Proof.

We refer to the Appendix
A
for a detailed proof.
∎

Lemma 4.10
.

Let
\Lambda\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}
be a finite set. Assume
t\leq s
,
\bm{l}\in P_{t}\setminus Q_{t}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}}^{s}\subset\Lambda
. If
\bm{u}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{l}}^{t}
and
\bm{v}\in\Lambda\setminus\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}}^{t}
, then there is some
\bm{u}^{\prime}\in\Lambda\setminus\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}}^{t}
such that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{u},\bm{v})|

\displaystyle\leq(\#\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}}^{t})\cdot e^{-\alpha_{t}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{u}-\bm{u}^{\prime}\|)}\cdot|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{u}^{\prime},\bm{v})|\cdot\delta_{t}^{-3}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{t}\left(1-\frac{6\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{t+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{u}-\bm{u}^{\prime}\|)}\cdot|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{u}^{\prime},\bm{v})|,

(4.176)

Proof.

The proof is similar to that of Lemma
4.9
, we omit the details.
∎

Remark 4.6
.

The second inequality is derived from
{\rm dist}(2\Omega_{\bm{l}}^{t},\Lambda\setminus\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}}^{t})\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{t}}{3}
and (
4.3
).

Proof of Theorem
4.8
.

We claim that
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
s
-good. In fact, for
s^{\prime}\leq s-1
, assume

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\Omega_{\bm{l}}^{s^{\prime}+1}\text{ and }\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}}^{s^{\prime}+1}\cap 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\neq\emptyset.

Thus by (
4
), we obtain

\displaystyle\Omega_{\bm{l}}^{s^{\prime}+1}\subset 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},

which contradicts

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}.

If there exists a
\bm{k}^{\prime}
such that
\bm{k}^{\prime}\in Q_{s}
and

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},

then by (
4.14
) and
{\rm dist}(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{u}}^{s+1},\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{u}^{\prime}}^{s+1})>10\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\ \text{for}\ \bm{u}\neq\bm{u}^{\prime}\in P_{s+1}
, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}.

This contradicts
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
. We have proven the claim.

Since (
4.18
) and
\bm{k}\notin Q_{s+1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|<\delta_{s}^{-2}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}<\delta_{s}^{-2}\delta_{s+1}^{-2}<\delta_{s+1}^{-3}.

(4.177)

To obtain the desired estimates, we divide the remaining proof into several cases.

Case 1
:

\bm{x}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
or
\bm{y}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
. Without loss of generality, we assume that
\bm{y}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
. From
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}}{10}
and
{\rm diam}(2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})\ll\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}
, we have
\bm{x}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
. Since
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
s
-good and (
4.175
), there is some
\bm{x}^{\prime}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
such that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq

\displaystyle\ (\#(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}))\cdot e^{-\alpha_{s}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime}\|)}

\displaystyle\cdot|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}(\bm{x}^{\prime},\bm{y})|\cdot\delta_{s}^{-3}.

(4.178)

Next, we will extract
\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)
from
\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime}\|)
. Since
\bm{x}^{\prime},\bm{y}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
,
\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}^{\prime}\|\leq 4\zeta_{s+1}
. From
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}}{10}\gg 4\zeta_{s+1}\gg 1
and (
3.2
), we have

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime}\|)\geq\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-4\zeta_{1})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-2\rho\frac{4\zeta_{s+1}}{(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)\log(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{80\rho\zeta_{s+1}}{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\log N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1).

(4.179)

Therefore, from (
4.177
), (
4.178
), (
4.179
),
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho<\rho^{\prime}+1
and
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}}{10}
, we get

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq(2\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}+1)^{d}\cdot e^{-\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{80\rho\zeta_{s+1}}{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\log N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)}\cdot\delta_{s}^{-5}\cdot\delta_{s+1}^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{6\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)}.

We complete the proof in this case.

Case 2
:

\bm{x}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
and
\bm{y}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
. In this case, for
1\leq t\leq s+1
, we define

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{t}=\{\bm{l}\in P_{t}:\ \exists\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Q_{t-1}\ {\rm s.t.}\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}^{\prime}}^{t-1}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}^{\prime}}^{t-1}\subset\Omega_{\bm{l}}^{t}\}.

(4.180)

From (
4
), (
4.14
) and (
4.180
), it follows that for
\bm{l}^{\prime}\in\widetilde{P}_{t}\cap Q_{t}

(1\leq t\leq s)
, there is a
\bm{l}\in\widetilde{P}_{t+1}
such that

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}^{\prime}}^{t}\subset\Omega_{\bm{l}}^{t+1}.

Hence for any
\bm{z}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
, if

\displaystyle\bm{z}\in\bigcup_{\bm{l}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{l}}^{1},

then there exists a
t\in[1,s]
such that

\displaystyle\bm{z}\in\bigcup_{\bm{l}\in\widetilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t}}2\Omega_{\bm{l}}^{t}.

Therefore, for
\bm{z}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
, we can define

\displaystyle O(\bm{z})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{z}),&\text{if }\bm{z}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus\left(\bigcup_{\bm{l}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{l}}^{1}\right),\\
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}}^{t},&\text{if }\bm{z}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{l}}^{t}\text{ for some $\bm{l}\in\widetilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t}$}.\end{array}\right.

Moreover, for
\bm{z}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
, by Corollary
4.10
, we can define

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{z}}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\bm{z},&\bm{z}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\bigcup O(\bm{y}),\\
\bm{z}^{\prime},&\bm{z}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus\left(2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\bigcup O(\bm{y})\right).\end{array}\right.

Let
\bm{x}_{0}:=\bm{x}
and
\bm{x}_{l+1}=\hat{\bm{x}}_{l}
,
l\geq 0
. For given
\{\bm{x}_{l}\}_{l\in\mathbb{N}}
, we define
l_{1}\geq 1
to be the smallest integer so that
\bm{x}_{l_{1}}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\bigcup O(\bm{y})
. We then have

\displaystyle\bm{x}_{i}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus\left(2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\bigcup O(\bm{y})\right)\text{ for }0\leq i<l_{1}.

We also divide the discussion into 3 cases:

Case 2-1
:

l_{1}>\left[\frac{2\alpha\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)+6\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log^{\rho^{\prime}}(N_{s+1}+1)}{\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(\frac{N_{1}}{2}+1\right)}\right]+1:=l^{*}
. Since
\bm{x}_{i}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus\left(2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\bigcup O(\bm{y})\right)
for
0\leq i<l_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{x}_{i+1}=\bm{x}^{\prime}_{s+1}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\setminus O(\bm{x}_{i})\ \text{(cf. Corollary \ref{itepsqt})}

and then

\displaystyle\|\bm{x}_{i+1}-\bm{x}_{i}\|\geq\frac{N_{1}}{2}\text{ for $0\leq i<l_{1}$.}

Thus, from (
4.3
),(
4.34
), (
4.176
), (
4.177
) and

\displaystyle\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{6\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\geq\frac{\alpha}{2},

we get

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq\prod_{i=0}^{l^{*}-1}\left(e^{-\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{6\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}_{i+1}-\bm{x}_{i}\|+1)}\right)|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}(\bm{x}_{l^{*}},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\cdot l^{*}\log^{\rho}\left(\frac{N_{1}}{2}+1\right)}\delta_{s+1}^{-3}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\cdot l^{*}\log^{\rho}\left(\frac{N_{1}}{2}+1\right)}\cdot e^{3\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log^{\rho^{\prime}}(N_{s+1}+1)}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)}.

Case 2-2:

l_{1}\leq l^{*}
and
\bm{x}_{l_{1}}\in O(\bm{y})
. According to
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}}{10}
,
N_{s+1}\gg 1
and
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho
, we obtain

\displaystyle l^{*}

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{2\alpha\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)+6\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log^{\rho^{\prime}}(N_{s+1}+1)}{\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(\frac{N_{1}}{2}+1\right)}\right]+1

\displaystyle\leq\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|).

(4.181)

Then from (
3.1
), (
4.34
), (
4.176
), (
4.177
) and
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}}{10}
, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq\prod_{i=0}^{l_{1}-1}\left(e^{-\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{6\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}_{i+1}-\bm{x}_{i}\|+1)}\right)|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}(\bm{x}_{l_{1}},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{6\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\left(\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{x}\|+1)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}l_{1}\right)}\delta_{s+1}^{-3}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{6\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\left(\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{y}\|+1)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}l_{1}\right)}

\displaystyle\ \ \cdot e^{3\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log^{\rho^{\prime}}(N_{s+1}+1)}.

(4.182)

By
\bm{x}_{l_{1}}\in O(\bm{y})
,
\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{y}\|\leq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{2}
. Since
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}}{10}\gg\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{2}\gg 1
and (
3.2
), we can get

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{y}\|+1)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}\left(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{2}+1\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\rho\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)\log(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{10\rho\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\log N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1).

(4.183)

From (
4.181
), (
4.183
),
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}}{10}\gg 1
and
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho<\rho^{\prime}+1
, we get

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{y}\|+1)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}l_{1}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)

and then

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\left(\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{y}\|+1)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}l_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{3\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1).

(4.184)

Since
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}}{10}\gg N_{s+1}\gg 1
, (
4.182
) and (
4.184
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{10\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)}\cdot e^{3\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log^{\rho^{\prime}}(N_{s+1}+1)}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{20\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)}.

Case 2-3
:

l_{1}\leq l^{*}
and
\bm{x}_{l_{1}}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
. From a similar argument of (
4.182
), we obtain

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{6\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\left(\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{y}\|+1)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}l_{1}\right)}

\displaystyle\ \ \cdot|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}}^{-1}(\bm{x}_{l_{1}},\bm{y})|

(4.185)

By Lemma
4.9
(since
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
s
-good), there is some
\bm{y}^{\prime}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
such that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}(\bm{x}_{l_{1}},\bm{y})|\leq(\#\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})\cdot e^{-\alpha_{s}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{y}^{\prime}\|)}\cdot|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}}^{-1}(\bm{x}_{l_{1}},\bm{y}^{\prime})|\cdot\delta_{s}^{-3}.

(4.186)

According to
\bm{x}_{l_{1}},\bm{y}^{\prime}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
, we have
\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{y}^{\prime}\|\leq 4\zeta_{s+1}
. Since (
3.1
) and
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}}{10}\gg 1
,

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{x}\|+1)+\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{y}^{\prime}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log(1+\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{x}\|+\|\bm{y}-\bm{y}^{\prime}\|)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\|\bm{x}_{l_{1}}-\bm{y}^{\prime}\|)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-4\zeta_{s+1})-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2.

(4.187)

From (
4.71
),
\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\gg\zeta_{s}
,
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho<\rho^{\prime}+1
and
N_{s+1}\gg 1
, we also get

\displaystyle\ \ \log(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-4\zeta_{s+1})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{80\rho\zeta_{s+1}}{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\log N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1).

(4.188)

Combining (
4.69
), (
Case 2-3:
)–(
4.188
),
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho<\rho^{\prime}+1
and
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}}{10}\gg 1
gives

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{20\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|+1)}.

This finishes the proof.
∎

Step 3

:
Estimates of general
(s+1)
-good
\Lambda
.

We now finalize the verification of property
\mathscr{P}_{s+1}
.
First, we recall the precise definition of an
(s+1)
-
{\rm good}
set: a finite set
\Lambda\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}
is
(s+1)
-
{\rm good}
iff

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\bm{k}^{\prime}\in Q_{s^{\prime}},\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\Lambda,\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s^{\prime}+1}\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s^{\prime}+1}\subset\Lambda\ \text{for $s^{\prime}<s+1$},\\
\{\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda\}\cap Q_{s+1}=\emptyset.\end{array}\right.

To establish
\mathscr{P}_{s+1}
, we will synthesize three key analytical tools: (a) The
\ell^{2}
-norm estimates for
\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}
developed previously; (b) Schur’s test for bounding matrix operators; (c) The resolvent identity technique.

Theorem 4.11
.

If
\Lambda
is
(s+1)
-
{\rm good}
, then

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|\leq

\displaystyle\ 2\delta_{s}^{-3}\sup_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\ \delta_{s+1}^{-3}.

(4.191)

and

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|<e^{-\alpha_{s+1}\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|}\ \text{for $\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq 10\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}$}.

(4.192)

Proof.

First, we prove (
4.82
) by Schur’s test. Define

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{t}=\{\bm{k}\in P_{t}:\ \exists\bm{k}^{\prime}\in Q_{t-1}\ \text{s.t. }\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{t-1}\subset\Lambda,\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{t-1}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t}\},\ 1\leq t\leq s+1.

(4.193)

From (
4.14
), (
4.3
) and (
4.193
), it follows that for
\bm{k}^{\prime}\in\widetilde{P}_{t}\cap Q_{t}

(1\leq t\leq s)
, there exists a
\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{t+1}
such that

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{t}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t+1}

and

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{s+1}\cap Q_{s+1}=\emptyset.

Hence for any
\bm{w}\in\Lambda
, if

\displaystyle\bm{w}\in\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1},

then there exists a
t\in[1,s+1]
such that

\displaystyle\bm{w}\in\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t}.

(4.194)

For every
\bm{w}\in\Lambda
, define its block in
\Lambda
:

\displaystyle O(\bm{w})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{w})\cap\Lambda,&\text{if $\bm{w}\notin\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}$,}\\
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{t},&\text{if $\bm{w}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t}$ for some $\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t}$.}\end{array}\right.

Since
\Lambda
is
(s+1)
-good,
\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}
exist for all
\bm{x}\in\Lambda
. Hence we can define
\mathcal{L}
and
\mathcal{K}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y}),&\text{for $\bm{x}\in\Lambda$ and $\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})$,}\\
0,&\text{else,}\end{array}\right.

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\sum\limits_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})}\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{z})\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y}),&\text{for $\bm{x}\in\Lambda$ and $\bm{y}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{x})$,}\\
0,&\text{else}.\end{array}\right.

Direct computations shows

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}=\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\varepsilon\mathcal{K}.

The norm estimate (
4.191
) follows by applying an argument parallel to the proof of (
4.82
), with appropriate modifications for the
(s+1)
-scale case.

We estimate
|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|
first. We divide the discussion into three cases:

Case 1
:

\bm{x}\notin\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
and
\bm{y}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{x})
. In the same manner as (
4.93
), we can obtain

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\right)}.

(4.201)

Case 2
:

\bm{x}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t}
for some
\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t}
and
\bm{y}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{x})
. In this case, we define
X:=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{t}}{9}}(\bm{k})\subset O(\bm{x})
. Hence

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq({\rm I})+({\rm II}),

(4.202)

where

\displaystyle({\rm I})=\sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus X}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|

and

\displaystyle({\rm II})=\sum_{\bm{z}\in X}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|.

For
({\rm I})
, since (
1.2
), (
3.1
),
\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus X
(
\|\bm{z}-\bm{x}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{t}}{10}
),
\bm{y}\notin O(\bm{x})
(
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{t}}{3}
) and (
4.21
), we have

\displaystyle({\rm I})=

\displaystyle\ \ \sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus X}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus X}e^{-\alpha^{\prime}_{t-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{z}\|)}\cdot e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{y}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ e^{-\alpha^{\prime}_{t-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)+\alpha^{\prime}_{t-1}\cdot C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2}\sum_{\bm{z}\in O(\bm{x})\setminus X}e^{-\frac{1}{10}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{y}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)\cdot e^{-\alpha^{\prime}_{t-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)+\alpha^{\prime}_{t-1}\cdot C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \frac{1}{2}e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{t-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)},

(4.203)

where
D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)
is defined in (
2.1
). For
({\rm II})
, from (
1.2
), (
4.21
) and
\bm{k}\notin Q_{t}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{z}\in X}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|\leq\delta_{t-1}^{-2}\delta_{t}^{-2}\sum_{\bm{z}\in X}e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{y}\|)}.

(4.204)

By
\bm{z}\in X
,
\bm{y}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{x})
(
\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{t}}{3}\geq 2\|\bm{x}-\bm{z}\|
) and (
3.2
), we get

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{z}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|-\|\bm{x}-\bm{z}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-2\rho\frac{\|\bm{x}-\bm{z}\|}{(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|)\log(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|)}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \ \left(1-\frac{1}{\log N_{t}}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|).

(4.205)

Hence, combining (
4.204
), (
4.205
),
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{t}}{3}\gg 1
, (
4.3
) and
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho
gives

\displaystyle({\rm II})=

\displaystyle\ \ \sum_{\bm{z}\in X}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{z})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \delta_{t-1}^{-2}\delta_{t}^{-2}\cdot e^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|)}\cdot\sum_{\bm{z}\in X}e^{-\frac{1}{10}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{y}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \delta_{t-1}^{-2}\delta_{t}^{-2}\cdot D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)\cdot e^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{y}-\bm{x}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \frac{1}{2}e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{t-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)},

(4.206)

where
D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)
is defined in (
2.1
). From (
4.202
), (
4.203
) and (
4.206
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{t-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}.

(4.207)

Case 3
:

\bm{x}\in\Lambda
and
\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})
. From (
4.3
), we get

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|=0\leq e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{s}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}.

(4.208)

In summary, we obtain

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq e^{-\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{s}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\text{ for all $\bm{x},\bm{y}\in\Lambda$}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq D\left(\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{s}\right)<+\infty,

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{K}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq D\left(\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{s}\right)<+\infty,

where
D\left(\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{s}\right)
is defined in (
2.1
). By Schur’s test, we can get

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}\|\leq D\left(\frac{5}{8}\alpha^{\prime}_{s}\right)\leq D\left(\frac{5}{16}\alpha\right)<+\infty.

From
\varepsilon\ll 1
, we have
\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\varepsilon\mathcal{K}
is invertible and

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\varepsilon\mathcal{K})^{-1}\|\leq 2.

(4.209)

At this time,

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}=(\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\varepsilon\mathcal{K})^{-1}\mathcal{L}

(4.210)

exists. Then, we estimate
\|\mathcal{L}\|
in order to estimate
\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|
by using similar methods.

We deal with
\sup_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|
first. We divide the discussion into two cases:

Case 1
:

\bm{x}\in\Lambda\setminus\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
. From (
4.26
) and (
4.3
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|=

\displaystyle\ \sum_{\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq(\#O(\bm{x}))\cdot\|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\ (N_{1}+1)^{d}\cdot 2\kappa_{1}^{-1}\delta_{0}^{-2}<\delta_{0}^{-\frac{5}{2}}.

(4.211)

Case 2
:

\bm{x}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t}
for some
\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t}
. In this case, by (
4.20
) and (
4.3
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq(\#O(\bm{x}))\cdot\|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle<\delta_{t-1}^{-3}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}<\delta_{t}^{-\frac{5}{2}}.

(4.212)

Since
\theta,\theta_{1},\cdots,\theta_{s+1}\in\mathbb{D}_{R}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{i}\|\leq\sqrt{4R^{2}+\frac{1}{4}},\ 1\leq i\leq s+1,

and

\displaystyle\delta_{t}^{-3}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{t+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{t+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}>\delta_{t}^{-\frac{5}{2}}.

(4.213)

Combining (
4.211
)–(
4.213
), we have

\displaystyle\ \ \sup_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \delta_{s}^{-3}\sup_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right).

(4.214)

Now, we estimate
\sup_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|
. We again divide the discussion into two cases:

Case 1
:

\bm{y}\in\Lambda\setminus\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}
. In this case,

\displaystyle\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})\text{ iff }\bm{x}\in\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y}).

At this time, if
\bm{x}\in\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})
, then
\bm{x}\in\Lambda\setminus\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
(
O(\bm{x})=\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{x})
is
0
-good). Hence by (
4.3
), (
4.26
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|=

\displaystyle\ \sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ (\#(\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})))\cdot 2\kappa_{1}^{-1}\delta_{0}^{-2}<\delta_{0}^{-\frac{5}{2}}.

(4.215)

Case 2
:

\bm{y}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{t}
for some
\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t}
. In this case,

\displaystyle\bm{y}\in O(\bm{x})\text{ iff }\bm{x}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t}\bigcup\left(\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})\right).

Therefore, from (
4.3
), (
4.26
) and (
4.20
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\bm{x}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t}}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|+\sum_{\bm{x}\in\left(\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})\right)\setminus(2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t})}|\mathcal{T}_{O(\bm{x})}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq(\#(2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t}))\delta_{t-1}^{-2}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\ \ +(\#(\Lambda\cap\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{y})))\cdot 2\kappa_{1}^{-1}\delta_{0}^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{t-1}^{-3}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

(4.216)

Combining (
4.213
), (
4.215
) and (
4.216
) gives

\displaystyle\ \ \sup_{\bm{y}\in\Lambda}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\Lambda}|\mathcal{L}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ \delta_{s}^{-3}\sup_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right).

(4.217)

Then, by (
4.214
), (
4.217
) and Schur’s test we know

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}\|\leq\delta_{s}^{-3}\sup_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right).

(4.218)

Finally, from (
4.3
), (
4.209
), (
4.210
) and (
4.218
), we prove

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|\leq

\displaystyle\ 2\delta_{s}^{-3}\sup_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\ \delta_{s+1}^{-3}.

This completes the proof of (
4.191
).

We initiate the proof of the off-diagonal decay estimate stated in (
4.192
). Throughout this argument, we maintain the standing assumption that

\displaystyle\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq 10\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}.

The proof will proceed through an iterative scheme, leveraging the following key estimates (
4.34
) and (
4.175
). For
\bm{z}\in\Lambda
, from Corollary
4.4
and Lemma
4.10
, there exists
\bm{z}^{\prime}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{z})
(cf. (
4.3
)) such that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{z},\bm{y})|\leq e^{-\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{s}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{z}-\bm{z}^{\prime}\|)}\cdot|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{z}^{\prime},\bm{y})|,

(4.219)

where
\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{s}=\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{30\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)
.

Next, iterating (
4.219
) for
m\geq 2
steps leads to the following: there exist
\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{2},\cdots,\bm{x}_{m}\in\Lambda
such that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|\leq e^{-\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{s}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|)\right)}|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{x}_{m},\bm{y})|,

(4.220)

where
\bm{x}_{0}=\bm{x}
and
\bm{x}_{k+1}=\bm{x}^{\prime}
,
k=0,1,\cdots,m-1
. We define
n\geq 1
to be the smallest integer so that
\bm{x}_{n}\in O(\bm{y})
(cf. (
4.3
)). We then have
\bm{x}_{i}\notin O(\bm{y})
for
i=0,1,\cdots,n-1
. We divide the discussion into two cases:

Case 1
:

n\leq\frac{\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)+3\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log(N_{s+1}+1)}{\frac{\alpha}{2}\log^{\rho}\left(\frac{N_{1}}{2}+1\right)}
. Using Lemma
3.1
and the triangle inequality implies

\displaystyle\ \sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \log^{\rho}\left(1+\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|\right)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}n

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{n}-\bm{x}\|)-C(\rho)\log^{\rho}n.

(4.221)

Since
\bm{x}_{n}\in O(\bm{y})
and
{\rm diam}(O(\bm{y}))\leq\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{x}-\bm{x}_{n}\|\geq\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\|\bm{x}_{n}-\bm{y}\|\lambda\geq\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}.

By
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq 10\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\geq N_{s+1}\gg 1
and
1<\rho^{\prime}<\rho<\rho^{\prime}+1
, applying Lemma
3.2
with
x=\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|
and
y=\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}
gives

\displaystyle\ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{n}-\bm{x}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|-\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \left(1-2\rho\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}}{(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)\log(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \left(1-\frac{10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|).

(4.222)

Under the same conditions, we have

\displaystyle C(\rho)\log^{\rho}n

\displaystyle\leq C(\rho)\log^{\rho}(2\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|))\leq\log(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}.

(4.223)

Combining (
4.221
), (
4.222
) and (
4.223
) shows

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|)\geq\left(1-\frac{2\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|).

(4.224)

From
\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq 10\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\geq N_{s+1}\gg 1
, (
4.3
), (
4.23
), (
4.220
) and (
4.224
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{s}\left(1-\frac{2\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\delta_{s+1}^{-3}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha_{s+1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}.

Case 2
:

n>\frac{\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)+3\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log(N_{s+1}+1)}{\frac{\alpha}{2}\log^{\rho}\left(\frac{N_{1}}{2}+1\right)}
. In this case, from (
4.23
), (
4.220
),
\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|\geq\frac{N_{1}}{2}
(since
\bm{x}_{k+1}\in\Lambda\setminus O(\bm{x}_{k})
),
k=0,1\cdots,n-1
and

\displaystyle\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{s}=\alpha_{s}\left(1-\frac{30\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}}{\alpha\log^{\rho-\rho^{\prime}}N_{s+1}}\right)>\frac{\alpha}{2},

it follows that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{s}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}_{k+1}-\bm{x}_{k}\|)\right)}|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{x}_{n},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-n\cdot\alpha^{\prime\prime}_{s}\log^{\rho}\left(\frac{N_{1}}{2}+1\right)}e^{3\times 10^{5\rho^{\prime}}\log^{\rho}(N_{s+1}+1)}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\leq e^{-\alpha_{s+1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}.

This concludes the proof of Lemma
4.11
.
∎

5.
Arithmetic spectral localization

In this section, we will prove Theorem
1.2
by combining Green’s function estimates and the Shnol’s theorem.

Proof of Theorem
1.2
.

Let
\varepsilon_{0}
be given by Theorem
1.1
. Fix
\theta\in\mathbb{T}\setminus\Theta_{\tau}
. Let
E\in\sigma(\mathcal{H}(\theta))
be a generalized eigenvalue of
\mathcal{H}(\theta)
and
\psi=\{\psi(\bm{n})\}_{\bm{n}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}}\neq 0
be the corresponding generalized eigenfunction satisfying

\displaystyle|\psi(\bm{n})|\leq(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{d}.

From Shnol’s theorem (cf.
[
Han19
]
), it suffices to show that

\displaystyle|\psi(\bm{n})|<e^{-\frac{\alpha}{4\cdot 10^{6\rho}}\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{n}\|\right)}\text{ for $\|\bm{n}\|\gg 1$}

and
\psi\in\ell^{2}(\mathbb{Z}^{d})
. For this purpose, note first that there exists some
\tilde{s}\in\mathbb{N}
such that

\displaystyle\|2\theta+\bm{n}\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}>\frac{1}{\|n\|^{\tau}}\ \text{for all $n$ satisfying $\|n\|\geq N_{\tilde{s}}$}.

(5.1)

We claim that there exists some
s_{0}>0
such that for
s\geq s_{0}
,

\displaystyle\Lambda_{2N_{s}^{10^{4}}}\cap\left(\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in Q_{s}}\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\right)\neq\emptyset.

(5.2)

Otherwise, there exists a sequence
s_{i}\rightarrow+\infty
(as
i\rightarrow\infty
) such that

\displaystyle\Lambda_{2N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}}\cap\left(\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in Q_{s_{i}}}\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s_{i}}\right)=\emptyset.

(5.3)

Then we can enlarge
\tilde{\Lambda}_{N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}}
to
\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}
so that

\displaystyle\Lambda_{N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}}\subset\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}\subset\Lambda_{N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}+50N_{s_{i}}^{100}}

and

\displaystyle\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}\ \text{for $s^{\prime}\leq s_{i}$ and $\bm{k}\in P_{s^{\prime}}$}.

From (
5.3
), we have

\displaystyle\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}\cap\left(\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in Q_{s_{i}}}\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s_{i}}\right)=\emptyset,

which shows that
\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}
is
s_{i}
-good. Let
\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0}=\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}}\cap\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}
. Using Poisson’s identity yields for
\bm{n}\in\Lambda_{N_{s_{i}}}
,

\displaystyle|\psi(\bm{n})|\leq

\displaystyle\ \sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}^{-1}(\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{n}^{\prime},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|\cdot|\psi(\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ ({\rm I})+({\rm II}),

where

\displaystyle({\rm I})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}^{-1}(\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{n}^{\prime},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|\cdot|\psi(\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|,

\displaystyle({\rm II})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}\setminus\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}^{-1}(\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{n}^{\prime},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|\cdot|\psi(\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|.

For
({\rm I})
, we have by Theorem
1.1
, (
1.2
), and
|\psi(\bm{n})|\leq(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{d}
that

\displaystyle({\rm I})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}^{-1}\|\cdot e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}\cdot(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{s_{i}}^{-3}\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}\cdot(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}\|)^{d}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{s_{i}}^{-3}(1+N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}})^{d}\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}\cdot(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}.

Since
\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|\geq\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\gg 1
and (
2.1
) we get

\displaystyle\ \ \sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}\cdot(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\right)}\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}\cdot(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\right)}\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}e^{-\frac{1}{10}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\right)}\cdot(\#\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0})\cdot D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right).

Therefore,

\displaystyle({\rm I})\leq e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\right)}\cdot\delta_{s_{i}}^{-3}\cdot(1+N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}})^{2d}\cdot D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)\rightarrow 0\ \text{as $i\rightarrow\infty$}.

For
({\rm II})
, we also have by Theorem
1.1
,
\|\bm{n}-\bm{n}^{\prime}\|\geq\frac{1}{4}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\gg 10\tilde{\zeta}_{s_{i}}
,
\|\bm{n}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|\geq\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\gg 1
, (
1.2
), (
3.1
) and
|\psi(\bm{n})|\leq(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{d}
that

\displaystyle({\rm II})\leq

\displaystyle\ \sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}\setminus\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}-\bm{n}^{\prime}\|)}\cdot e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}\cdot(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}\setminus\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}\left(e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)+\frac{1}{2}\alpha C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2}\right.

\displaystyle\ \ \left.\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}\cdot(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{d}(1+\|\bm{n}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ (1+N_{s_{i}})^{d}e^{-\frac{1}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\right)}\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}\setminus\tilde{\Lambda}_{i,0},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ (1+N_{s_{i}})^{d}(1+2N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}})^{d}e^{-\frac{1}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\right)}\cdot D\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)\rightarrow 0\ \text{as $i\rightarrow\infty$}.

It follows that
\psi(\bm{n})=0\ {\rm for}\ \forall\bm{n}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}
, which contradicts
\psi\neq 0
. The Claim is proved.

Next define

\displaystyle U_{s}=\Lambda_{8N_{s+1}^{10^{4}}}\setminus\Lambda_{4N_{s}^{10^{4}}},\ U_{s}^{*}=\Lambda_{10N_{s+1}^{10^{4}}}\setminus\Lambda_{3N_{s}^{10^{4}}}.

We can also enlarge
U_{s}^{*}
to
\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}
so that

\displaystyle U_{s}^{*}\subset\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}\subset\Lambda_{50N_{s}^{100}}(U_{s}^{*})

and

\displaystyle\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}\ \text{for $s^{\prime}\leq s$ and $\bm{k}\in P_{s^{\prime}}$}.

Let
\bm{n}
satisfy
\|\bm{n}\|>\max(4N_{\tilde{s}}^{10^{4}},4N_{s_{0}}^{10^{4}})
. Then there exists some
s\geq\max(\tilde{s},s_{0})
such that

\displaystyle\bm{n}\in U_{s}\subset\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}.

(5.4)

By virtue of condition (
5.2
) and without loss of generality, we may restrict our consideration to the case where there exists a
\bm{k}\in Q_{s}^{+}
such that

\displaystyle\Lambda_{2N_{s}^{10^{4}}}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\neq\emptyset.

Then for
\bm{k}\neq\bm{k}^{\prime}\in Q_{s}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime}\|>\left(\frac{\gamma}{2\delta_{s}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tau}}\gg N_{s+1}^{10^{5}}\gg{\rm diam}(\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}\cap\left(\bigcup_{\bm{l}\in Q_{s}^{+}}\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}}^{s}\right)=\emptyset.

Now, if there exists some
\bm{l}\in Q_{s}^{-}
such that

\displaystyle\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}}^{s}\neq\emptyset,

then

\displaystyle 2N_{s}^{10^{4}}-100N_{s}^{100}\leq\|\bm{l}\|-\|\bm{k}\|\leq\|\bm{l}+\bm{k}\|\leq\|\bm{l}\|+\|\bm{k}\|<11N_{s+1}^{10^{4}}.

Recalling

\displaystyle Q_{s}\subset P_{s}\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i},

we have
\bm{l}+\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}
. According to (
5.1
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(11N_{s+1}^{10^{4}})^{\tau}}<\|2\theta+(\bm{l}+\bm{k})\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\leq\|\theta+\bm{l}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}+\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<2\delta_{s},

which contradicts

\displaystyle\delta_{s}^{-1}\gg N_{s+1}^{10^{4}\tau}.

We thus have shown

\displaystyle\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}\cap\left(\bigcup_{\bm{l}\in Q_{s}}\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{l}}^{s}\right)=\emptyset.

This implies that
\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}
is
s
-
{\rm good}
.

Finally, by recalling (
5.4
), we can set

\displaystyle\hat{U}_{s}=\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{s}^{10^{4}}}(U_{s}).

Then

\displaystyle|\psi(\bm{n})|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{U}_{s}^{*},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}^{-1}(\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{n}^{\prime},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|\cdot|\psi(\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle({\rm III})+({\rm IV}),

where

\displaystyle({\rm III})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\hat{U}_{s},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}^{-1}(\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{n}^{\prime},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|\cdot|\psi(\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|,

\displaystyle({\rm IV})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}\setminus\hat{U}_{s},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}^{-1}(\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{n}^{\prime},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|\cdot|\psi(\bm{n}^{\prime\prime})|.

For
({\rm III})
, we have by Theorem
1.1
, (
1.2
), and
|\psi(\bm{n})|\leq(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{d}
that

\displaystyle({\rm III})\leq

\displaystyle\ \sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\hat{U}_{s},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}^{-1}\|\cdot e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}\cdot(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \delta_{s}^{-3}\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\hat{U}_{s},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}\|)^{d}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \delta_{s}^{-3}(1+9N_{s+1}^{10^{4}})^{d}\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\hat{U}_{s},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}.

Since
\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|\geq\frac{1}{4}N_{s}^{10^{4}}\gg 1
and (
2.1
) we get

\displaystyle\ \ \sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\hat{U}_{s},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{4}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\right)}\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\hat{U}_{s},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}\cdot(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{4}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\right)}\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\hat{U}_{s},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}e^{-\frac{1}{10}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \ e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\right)}\cdot(\#\hat{U}_{s})\cdot D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right).

Therefore,

\displaystyle({\rm III})\leq

\displaystyle\ e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{4}N_{s}^{10^{4}}\right)}\cdot\delta_{s}^{-3}\cdot(1+9N_{s+1}^{10^{4}})^{2d}\cdot D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{2}e^{-\frac{1}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{4}N_{s}^{10^{4}}\right)}.

For
({\rm IV})
, we also have by Theorem
1.1
,
\|\bm{n}-\bm{n}^{\prime}\|\geq\frac{1}{4}N_{s}^{10^{4}}\gg 10\tilde{\zeta}_{s}
,
\|\bm{n}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|\geq\frac{1}{2}N_{s}^{10^{4}}\gg 1
, (
1.2
), (
3.1
) and
|\psi(\bm{n})|\leq(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{d}
that

\displaystyle({\rm IV})\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}\setminus\hat{U}_{s},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}-\bm{n}^{\prime}\|)}\cdot e^{-\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}\cdot(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}\setminus\hat{U}_{s},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}\left(e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)+\frac{1}{2}\alpha C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2}\right.

\displaystyle\ \ \left.\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}\cdot(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{d}(1+\|\bm{n}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)^{d}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+9N_{s+1}^{10^{4}})^{d}e^{-\frac{1}{3}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\right)}\sum_{\bm{n}^{\prime}\in\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}\setminus\hat{U}_{s},\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\notin\tilde{U}_{s}^{*}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}^{\prime}-\bm{n}^{\prime\prime}\|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+9N_{s+1}^{10^{4}})^{d}(1+11N_{s+1}^{10^{4}})^{d}e^{-\frac{1}{3}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{2}N_{s_{i}}^{10^{4}}\right)}\cdot D\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}e^{-\frac{1}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{4}N_{s}^{10^{4}}\right)}.

Hence,

\displaystyle|\psi(\bm{n})|\leq e^{-\frac{1}{4}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{4}N_{s}^{10^{4}}\right)}.

Combining the above estimates and
\|\bm{n}\|\leq 8N_{s+1}^{10^{4}}
, we have

\displaystyle|\psi(\bm{n})|<e^{-\frac{\alpha}{4\cdot 10^{6\rho}}\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{n}\|\right)}.

We complete the proof of arithmetic spectral localization.
∎

6.
Sub-polynomial bounds of moments

For convenience, we will divide this proof into several parts.

6.1.
Basic assumptions

There are some assumption used throughout the proof:

(1)

1<\frac{1+\rho^{\prime}}{2}<\rho^{\prime}<\rho<\rho^{\prime}+1
.

(2)

\mathcal{H}(\theta)
is self-adjoint for all
\theta\in\mathbb{T}
, which implies
v
is real on
\mathbb{T}
.

(3)

v(\mathbb{T})=[a,b]
, since
v
is continuous on
\mathbb{T}
.

(4)

There is some
\beta>0
such that
\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ \Re z\in[a-2\beta,b+2\beta],\ \Im z\in[-\beta,\beta]\}\subset v(\mathbb{D}_{R/2})
by using open mapping theorem and
v
is analytic on
\mathbb{D}_{R}
.

(5)

\varepsilon
is sufficiently small such that
\sigma(\mathcal{H}(\theta))\subset[a-\beta,b+\beta]
for all
\theta\in\mathbb{T}
and
|\varepsilon|\cdot 2e^{\frac{19}{20}\alpha C(\rho)\log^{\rho}2}\cdot D\left(\frac{\alpha}{20}\right)<\frac{\beta}{2}
, where
D(\alpha)
is defined in (
2.1
).

(6)

E\in\mathbb{R}
.

6.2.
Connection between moments and Green’s functions

In this part, we will establish a connection between moments and Green’s functions. We have

Lemma 6.1
.

For all
\theta\in\mathbb{T},t,{T}>0,
and
\bm{n}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\ |\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{(b-a+4\beta)e^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\int_{a-2\beta}^{b+2\beta}\left|\left\langle\left(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-E-\sqrt{-1}t^{-1}\right)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\right\rangle\right|^{2}dE

\displaystyle\ +\frac{2e^{2}}{\beta^{2}\pi^{2}}(b-a+6\beta+2t^{-1})^{2}e^{-\frac{9}{5}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)}.

(6.1)

and

\displaystyle\ \frac{2}{T}\int^{\infty}_{0}e^{-2t/T}|\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|^{2}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{T\pi}\int_{a-2\beta}^{b+2\beta}|\langle(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-E-\sqrt{-1}t)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|^{2}dE+\frac{4}{\beta T\pi}e^{-\frac{9}{5}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)}.

(6.2)

Proof.

We first deal with (
6.1
). Given
t>0
, we will consider the following contour
\mathscr{C}=\mathscr{C}_{1}\cup\mathscr{C}_{2}\cup\mathscr{C}_{3}\cup\mathscr{C}_{4}
, where

\displaystyle\mathscr{C}_{1}

\displaystyle=\{z=E+\sqrt{-1}y:\ E\in[a-2\beta,b+2\beta],\ y=t^{-1}\},

\displaystyle\mathscr{C}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{z=E+\sqrt{-1}y:\ E=a-2\beta,\ y\in[-\beta,t^{-1}]\},

\displaystyle\mathscr{C}_{3}

\displaystyle=\{z=E+\sqrt{-1}y:\ E\in[a-2\beta,b+2\beta],\ y=-\beta\},

\displaystyle\mathscr{C}_{4}

\displaystyle=\{z=E+\sqrt{-1}y:\ E=b+2\beta,\ y\in[-\beta,t^{-1}]\}.

By Cauchy integral formula, we can get

\displaystyle\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle=-\frac{1}{2\pi\sqrt{-1}}\int_{\mathscr{C}}e^{-\sqrt{-1}tz}\langle(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-z)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle dz.

Notice that for
z\in\mathscr{C}
, we have
\Im z\leq t^{-1}
and hence
|e^{-\sqrt{-1}tz}|=e^{t\Im z}\leq e
. Thus

\displaystyle|\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|\leq\frac{e}{2\pi}\sum_{j=1}^{4}\int_{\mathscr{C}_{j}}|\langle(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-z)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|\cdot|dz|.

(6.3)

If
z\in\mathscr{C}_{2}\cup\mathscr{C}_{3}\cup\mathscr{C}_{4}
, then
{\rm dist}(z,\sigma(\mathcal{H}(\theta)))\geq\beta
and hence Lemma
3.4
shows that

\displaystyle\ \sum_{j=2}^{4}\int_{\mathscr{C}_{j}}|\langle(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-z)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|\cdot|dz|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{2}{\beta}(b-a+6\beta+2t^{-1})e^{-\frac{9}{10}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)}.

(6.4)

Using the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\ \left(\int_{\mathscr{C}_{1}}|\langle(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-z)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|\cdot|dz|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ (b-a+4\beta)\cdot\int_{a-2\beta}^{b+2\beta}|\langle(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-E-\sqrt{-1}t^{-1})^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|^{2}dE,

which combines
(x+y)^{2}\leq 2(x^{2}+y^{2})
, (
6.3
) and (
6.4
) implying that

\displaystyle\ |\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{(b-a+4\beta)e^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\int_{a-2\beta}^{b+2\beta}\left|\left\langle\left(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-E-\sqrt{-1}t^{-1}\right)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\right\rangle\right|^{2}dE

\displaystyle\ \ +\frac{2e^{2}}{\beta^{2}\pi^{2}}(b-a+6\beta+2t^{-1})^{2}e^{-\frac{9}{5}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)}.

We then analyze (
6.1
). Let

\displaystyle f(t):=e^{-\frac{t}{T}}\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle\chi_{[0,\infty)}(t),

where
\chi_{[0,\infty)}
denotes the characteristic function of
[0,\infty)
. Its Fourier transform is given by

\displaystyle\hat{f}(-E)

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}e^{\sqrt{-1}tE}f(t)dt

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-t(T^{-1}-\sqrt{-1}E)}\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}e^{-\sqrt{-1}tu}\mu_{\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}}(du)\right)dt

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-t(T^{-1}+\sqrt{-1}(u-E))}dt\right)\mu_{\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}}(du)

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{-\sqrt{-1}}{u-E-\sqrt{-1}T^{-1}}\mu_{\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}}(du)

\displaystyle=-\sqrt{-1}\left\langle\left(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-E-\sqrt{-1}T^{-1}\right)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\right\rangle,

where Fubini’s theorem is used and
\mu_{\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}}
is the spectral measure associated with
\mathcal{H}(\theta)
and
\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}
. Thus, by Parseval’s theorem on the Fourier transform,

\displaystyle\ \frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{\frac{-2t}{T}}|\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|^{2}dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{T\pi}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\left|\left\langle\left(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-E-\sqrt{-1}T^{-1}\right)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\right\rangle\right|^{2}dE.

Recall that
\sigma(\mathcal{H}(\theta))\subset[a-\beta,b+\beta]
. By Lemma
3.4
, for any
E\in(b+2\beta,\infty)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\left(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-E-\sqrt{-1}T^{-1}\right)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\right\rangle\right|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\left(E-b-\frac{3}{2}\beta\right)^{2}}e^{-\frac{9}{5}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)}.

Similarly, for any
E\in(-\infty,a-2\beta)
, we also have

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\left(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-E-\sqrt{-1}T^{-1}\right)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\right\rangle\right|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\left(E-a+\frac{3}{2}\beta\right)^{2}}e^{-\frac{9}{5}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\ \frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{\frac{-2t}{T}}|\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|^{2}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{T\pi}\int_{a-2\beta}^{b+2\beta}\left|\left\langle\left(\mathcal{H}(\theta)-E-\sqrt{-1}T^{-1}\right)^{-1}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\right\rangle\right|^{2}dE

\displaystyle\ +\frac{4}{\beta T\pi}e^{-\frac{9}{5}\alpha\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)}.

We complete this proof.
∎

6.3.
Green’s function estimate

Because of subsection
6.2
, we only need to bound Green’s functions. We first have the following conclusion

Lemma 6.2
.

If
a-2\beta\leq E\leq b+2\beta
,
\delta_{s}^{3}\leq t^{-1}\leq\beta
, then for
\theta\in\mathbb{T}
and any
\bm{k}\in P_{s}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}}^{-1}(E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1};\theta)\|

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{s}^{-3}

(6.5)

and

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}}^{-1}(E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1};\theta)(\bm{x},\bm{y})|

\displaystyle<e^{-\alpha^{\prime}_{s-1}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\ \text{for $\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s-1}}{10}$}.

(6.6)

Remark 6.1
.

Strictly speaking, the definitions of both
P_{s}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}
depend on the energe
E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1}
. For notational simplicity, we may suppress this dependence in our notation. We adopt the same notational convention for subsequent concepts
s
-regular sets and
s
-good sets.

Remark 6.2
.

We demand
a-2\beta\leq E\leq b+2\beta
,
t^{-1}\leq\beta
only for
E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1}\in v(\mathbb{D}_{R/2})
, which meets the condition of Theorem
1.1
holds. If (
6.5
) is established, the proof of (
6.6
) is similar to Theorem
4.8
, we omit the related details.

Proof.

Since
\mathcal{H}(\theta)
is self-adjoint and
\delta_{s}^{3}\leq t^{-1}\leq\beta
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}}^{-1}(E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1};\theta)\|=\frac{1}{{\rm dist}(E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1},\sigma(\mathcal{H}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}}(\theta)))}\leq\delta_{s}^{-3}.

We finish this proof.
∎

Next, we need to estimate Green’s function of more general sets. Assume that the finite set
\Lambda
is
s
-regular, namely

\displaystyle\Lambda\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s^{\prime}}\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\Lambda\ \text{for}\ s^{\prime}\leq s\ \text{and}\ \bm{k}\in P_{s^{\prime}}.

(6.7)

We have

Lemma 6.3
.

Under the assumptions of Lemma
6.2
, if
\Lambda
is
s
-regular, then for
\theta\in\mathbb{T}
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1};\theta)\|\leq\delta_{s}^{-3},

(6.8)

and

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1};\theta)(\bm{x},\bm{y})|<e^{-\alpha_{s}\log^{\rho}(1+\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|)}\text{ for $\|\bm{x}-\bm{y}\|>10\tilde{\zeta}_{s}$}.

(6.9)

Proof.

From self-adjointness of
\mathcal{H}(\theta)
and
\delta_{s}^{3}\leq t^{-1}\leq\beta
, we get

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1};\theta)\|=\frac{1}{{\rm dist}(E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1},\sigma(\mathcal{H}_{\Lambda}(\theta)))}\leq\delta_{s}^{-3}.

Define

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{t}=\{\bm{k}\in P_{t}:\ \exists\bm{k}^{\prime}\in Q_{t-1}\ {\rm s.t.},\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{t-1}\subset\Lambda,\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{t-1}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t}\}\ (1\leq t\leq s).

(6.10)

Similar to (
4.194
), for any
\bm{l}\in\Lambda
, if

\displaystyle\bm{l}\in\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\tilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1},

then there exists
1\leq t\leq s-1
such that

\displaystyle\bm{l}\in\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\tilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t}

or

\displaystyle\bm{l}\in\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\tilde{P}_{s}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s}.

For every
\bm{l}\in\Lambda
, define

\displaystyle O(\bm{l})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{l})\cap\Lambda,&\text{if }\bm{l}\notin\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\tilde{P}_{1}}2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1},\\
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{t},&\text{if }\bm{l}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{t}\ \text{for some }\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t},\\
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s},&\text{if }\bm{l}\in 2\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s}\ \text{for some }\bm{k}\in\widetilde{P}_{s}.\end{array}\right.

In light of (
4.20
), (
4.21
), Lemma
4.3
, Lemma
6.2
and (
6.7
), the demonstration for (
6.9
) parallels that of (
4.174
), thus we suppress the routine details.
∎

Now, we can state our main conclusion of this part:

Theorem 6.4
.

If
a-2\beta\leq E\leq b+2\beta
and
\delta_{s}^{3}\leq t^{-1}<\min(\delta_{s-1}^{3},\beta)
, then for
\theta\in\mathbb{T}
and any
\bm{n}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}
with
\|\bm{n}\|\geq e^{(\log t)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}\gg N_{s}^{10^{3}}
, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}^{-1}(E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1};\theta)(\bm{0},\bm{n})|<e^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha_{s}\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{n}\|\right)}.

Proof.

Since
\mathcal{H}(\theta)
is self-adjoint, one has that for any
\Lambda\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}
,
{\rm dist}(\sigma(\mathcal{H}_{\Lambda}(\theta)),E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1})\geq t^{-1}
, and hence,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1};\theta)\|\leq t\leq\delta_{s}^{-3}.

(6.11)

By Lemma
A.4
, for fixed
\bm{n}
with
\|\bm{n}\|\geq e^{(\log t)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}\gg N_{s}^{10^{3}}
, there exists a
s
-regular set
O_{\bm{n}}
satisfying

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\frac{1}{5}\|\bm{n}\|}(\bm{n})\subset O_{\bm{n}}\subset\Lambda_{\frac{1}{5}\|\bm{n}\|+50N_{s}^{100}}(\bm{n}).

For simplicity, we omit the dependence of
\mathcal{T}(E+\sqrt{-1}t^{-1};\theta)
on
E,t,\theta
. Using resolvent identity shows that

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{-1}(\bm{0},\bm{n})=-\sum_{\bm{l}\notin O_{\bm{n}},\bm{l}^{\prime}\in O_{\bm{n}}}\mathcal{T}^{-1}(\bm{0},\bm{l})\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})\mathcal{T}_{O_{\bm{n}}}^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n}).

Let
O_{\bm{n},0}=\Lambda_{\frac{1}{10}\|\bm{n}\|}(\bm{n})\cap O_{\bm{n}}
, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}^{-1}(\bm{0},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\bm{l}\notin O_{\bm{n}},\bm{l}^{\prime}\in O_{\bm{n}}}|\mathcal{T}^{-1}(\bm{0},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|\mathcal{T}_{O_{\bm{n}}}^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle=({\rm I})+({\rm II}),

where

\displaystyle({\rm I})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{l}\notin O_{\bm{n}},\bm{l}^{\prime}\in O_{\bm{n},0}}|\mathcal{T}^{-1}(\bm{0},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|\mathcal{T}_{O_{\bm{n}}}^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|,

\displaystyle({\rm II})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{l}\notin O_{\bm{n}},\bm{l}^{\prime}\in O_{\bm{n}}\setminus O_{\bm{n},0}}|\mathcal{T}^{-1}(\bm{0},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|\mathcal{T}_{O_{\bm{n}}}^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|.

For
({\rm I})
, we have by (
1.2
), (
6.11
) and
\|\bm{l}-\bm{l}^{\prime}\|\geq\frac{1}{10}\|\bm{n}\|
that

\displaystyle({\rm I})

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{T}^{-1}\|\cdot\|\mathcal{T}_{O_{\bm{n}}}^{-1}\|\cdot(\#O_{\bm{n},0})\cdot D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)e^{-\frac{9}{10}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{10}\|\bm{n}\|\right)}.

For
({\rm II})
, we also have by (
3.1
), (
6.11
) and (
6.9
) that

\displaystyle({\rm II})

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{T}^{-1}\|\cdot(\#O_{\bm{n}})\cdot D\left(\frac{\alpha}{10}\right)e^{-\alpha_{s}\log^{\rho}\left(1+\frac{1}{10}\|\bm{n}\|\right)+C(\rho)\alpha_{s}\log^{\rho}2}.

Therefore, since Lemma
6.3
and
t^{-1}<\delta_{s-1}^{3}
, we can obtain

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}^{-1}(\bm{0},\bm{n})|<e^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha_{s}\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{n}\|\right)}.

We finish this proof.
∎

6.4.
The completion of proof

In this part, we will combine the previous content to complete the proof of Theorem
1.3
.

Proof of Theorem
1.3
.

Let
T_{0}=\max\{\beta^{-1},\delta_{0}^{-3}\}
. If
t\geq T_{0}
, there is some
s\geq 1
such that

\displaystyle\delta_{s}^{3}\leq t^{-1}<\min(\delta_{s-1}^{3},\beta).

We let

\displaystyle\left(|\mathscr{X}_{\mathcal{H}(\theta)}|_{\delta_{\bm{0}}}^{p}\right)(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{n}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{p}\left|\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=({\rm I})+({\rm II}),

where

\displaystyle({\rm I})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\|\bm{n}\|\leq e^{(\log t)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{p}\left|\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle\right|^{2},

\displaystyle({\rm II})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\|\bm{n}\|>e^{(\log t)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{p}\left|\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle\right|^{2}.

For
({\rm I})
, direct computation shows

\displaystyle({\rm I})\leq

\displaystyle\ (1+e^{(\log t)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}})^{p}\sum_{\|\bm{n}\|\leq e^{(\log t)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}}\left|\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ (1+e^{(\log t)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}})^{p}.

For
({\rm II})
, by Lemma
6.1
and Theorem
6.4
, we have

\displaystyle({\rm II})\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\|\bm{n}\|>e^{(\log t)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}}\frac{(b-a+4\beta)^{2}e^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{p}e^{-\frac{3}{2}\alpha_{s}\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{n}\|\right)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\|\bm{n}\|>e^{(\log t)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}}\frac{2e^{2}}{\beta^{2}\pi^{2}}(b-a+6\beta+2t^{-1})^{2}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{p}e^{-\frac{9}{5}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{n}\|\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1.

Hence, we get

\displaystyle\left(|\mathscr{X}_{\mathcal{H}(\theta)}|_{\delta_{\bm{0}}}^{p}\right)(t)\leq 2^{p}e^{p(\log t)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}.

Let
a(\bm{n},T)=\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{\frac{-2t}{T}}|\langle e^{-\sqrt{-1}t\mathcal{H}(\theta)}\delta_{\bm{0}},\delta_{\bm{n}}\rangle|^{2}dt
, then

\displaystyle\left(|\bar{\mathscr{X}}_{\mathcal{H}(\theta)}|_{\delta_{\bm{0}}}^{p}\right)(T)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{n}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{p}a(\bm{n},T)

\displaystyle=({\rm III})+({\rm IV}),

where

\displaystyle({\rm III})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\|\bm{n}\|\leq e^{(\log T)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{p}a(\bm{n},T),

\displaystyle({\rm IV})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\|\bm{n}\|>e^{(\log T)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{p}a(\bm{n},T).

For
({\rm III})
, direct computation shows

\displaystyle({\rm III})\leq

\displaystyle\ (1+e^{(\log T)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}})^{p}\sum_{\|\bm{n}\|\leq e^{(\log T)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}}a(\bm{n},T)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ (1+e^{(\log T)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}})^{p}.

For
({\rm IV})
, by Lemma
6.1
and Theorem
6.4
, we also have

\displaystyle({\rm IV})\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\|\bm{n}\|>e^{(\log T)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}}(1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{p}e^{-\frac{3}{2}\alpha_{s}\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{n}\|\right)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\|\bm{n}\|>e^{(\log T)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}}\frac{4}{\beta\pi}((1+\|\bm{n}\|)^{p}e^{-\frac{9}{5}\alpha\log^{\rho}\left(1+\|\bm{n}\|\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1.

Therefore, we also obtain

\displaystyle\left(|\bar{\mathscr{X}}_{\mathcal{H}(\theta)}|_{\delta_{\bm{0}}}^{p}\right)(T)\leq 2^{p}e^{p(\log T)^{\frac{2}{1+\rho^{\prime}}}}.

We complete this proof.
∎
